Insertion 1：量產測試確定導入，測試時間有望大幅縮減
資料來源：Advantest、致茂、光焱、汎銓、TrendForce 、福邦投顧預估整理
PIC耦光對位為測試瓶頸 ，漏光預篩可望成為提速關鍵： Ins1於晶圓階段對EIC與PIC進行CP測試，EIC沿用一
般電性Probing，PIC則需在Prober加裝光學對位模組，量測光路損耗與MRM、PD等元件特性。由於每顆die都
須以次微米精度重新對準光纖，測試耗時成為量產瓶頸。業界正導入漏光定位設備作為預篩，先剔除漏光或有
潛在缺陷的die，免跑完整量測，以縮短整片晶圓的總測試時間 ；其中光焱NightJar 單格掃描不到1秒，量產版
NightJar-H正於台積電進行HVM驗證。
台廠方面：光焱可望受惠於預篩設備導入，致茂則以PIC整機方案送驗中，若通過驗證可望切入Insertion 1。
圖14、PIC檢測機台示意圖 圖15、PIC/EIC晶圓測試重點整理
項目
測試內容 EIC：同一般電性ProbingPIC：Prober加裝光學對位模組，打光量測損耗

核心目的 檢測光路損耗（耦合器、波導）及MRM、濾波元件、PD等元件特性
核心技術 6軸平台夾持光纖探針、可調雷射＋光功率計、波長掃描找微環共振；選配紅外線漏光定位
主要瓶頸 每顆die需重新對準光纖，次微米級閉環耦合，耗時過長
關鍵供應鏈 測試機：Teradyne、Advantest；探針台/卡：TEL、FormFactor；6軸平台：PI；光學量測：Keysight

### Page 18

18【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Insertion 2：技術尚未成熟仍待定，去留成觀察重點
資料來源：Advantest、Teradyne、旺矽、致茂、光焱、漢測、TrendForce ；福邦投顧整理
Ins2為CPO測試最難一站，雙面架構拖慢產出：Ins2於EIC以SoIC混合鍵合至PIC晶圓後、切割前進行測試，以上
電下光方式量測光迴路 、電光轉換之間的各種參數 。由於測試價值隨階段往後遞增 ，提前剔除不良die可提升後
續封裝良率，並劃分晶圓廠與OSAT之間的良率責任。此站需一面電性接觸、一面光耦合並同步精準對位 ，並兼
顧EIC發熱對Micro-Ring的影響 ，雙面架構難以多site平行測試，現行方案多為單site，單片晶圓測試約需6小時以
上，因耗時過長部分業者傾向暫時跳過，2027年放量看法分歧。
台廠方面：Advantest + 旺矽持續出二代機驗證中，漢測主要幫ficonTEC代工，光焱則提供快篩替代方案。
圖16、EIC/PIC D2W晶粒示意圖 圖17、EIC+PIC鍵合後晶圓測試重點整理

項目
測試內容 EIC以SoIC鍵合至PIC晶圓後、切割前，上電下光測E/O、O/E、O/O、光迴路及S參數
核心目的 測試價值隨階段遞增，提前剔除不良die以提升後續封裝良率
核心技術 雙面探測（一面電性接觸、一面光耦合）並同步對位；晶粒級溫控，避免EIC發熱影響微環共振
主要瓶頸 難以多site平行測試，多為單site、產出低；光耦合易受震動、溫度與翹曲影響
關鍵供應鏈 Teradyne＋ficonTEC（第二版驗證中）；Advantest＋旺矽（工程機已出貨，量產驗證中）

Insertion 3：Die Level全檢不可省，Shift to middle成CPO測試長期主力
資料來源：致茂、穎崴、萬潤、漢測、TrendForce；福邦投顧整理
Insertion 3為Die Level全檢，單顆取放拉長測試時間： Insertion 3於OE切割並貼合receptacle後進行測試，量測
光路品質（IL、PDL）與loopback誤碼率（BER），目的是避免切割、貼合過程產生的不良OE流入後續載板黏
合製程。相較Insertion 2的Wafer Level測試，Insertion 3須單顆取放、溫控與對接並全數檢測，未來可能每顆OE
的測試時間約為Insertion 2 per die的2–3倍。量產前全檢為必要條件，部分流程已導入大功率與老化測試，測試
時間仍有上升空間，但未來可能往多site平行測試發展以提升產出。
台廠方面：漢測（ficonTEC驗證中）、致茂(Tester出量產機)，萬潤則為FAU / receptacle耦合貼合主要供應商。

Insertion 4：Switch ASIC+OE成品測試，SLT為最後一塊拼圖

（加測回送誤碼率 ），以及在接近實際運作狀態下跑真實流量的 SLT。預計未來將加入光電同測 、SLT，目前
Ins4eo連測試參數都尚未完全確定 。測試難點在於CPO元件尺寸大、功耗高，FAU設計不統一，散熱頭與光學對
接存在空間衝突，且ASIC與OE需雙區溫控；SLT更需供應大量外部雷射光源，測試複雜度仍高。

資料來源：各公司、福邦投顧整理預估
TAM往後段集中 ，Ins3與Ins4為主要成長動能： 隨OE需求在2028年持續放大 ，測試設備 TAM明顯成長 ，其中
Insertion 3與Insertion 4的成長幅度皆高於整體，2028年合計占逾7成。Insertion 3須逐顆取放並全數檢測，是確保
Known Good OE的最後關卡；Insertion 4則須驗證整顆CPO封裝的光電功能，並逐步導入SLT，單顆測試價值最高
。若業界跳過Insertion 2，原本在晶圓階段的測試需求也將移往Insertion 3，進一步推升後段測試需求。

圖24、每產出一顆良品OE所需測試時間比較
資料來源：福邦投顧整理預估
SoIC 良率越高，不做 Ins2 每顆良品 OE 所需測試時間越少：不做 Ins 2 可省下每顆 die 在 Ins 2 的 45 秒，代價是
SoIC 不良品會額外占用 Ins 3 的測試時間，因此節省幅度取決於 SoIC 良率。良率約 60% 時兩者打平；良率越高
、不良品越少，每產出一顆良品 OE 所需的測試時間由良率 60% 時的 187.5 秒降至 80%/90% 時的 140.6/125.0 秒
，相較有做 Ins 2 分別節省約 28/38 秒（-17%/-23%）。
整體測試設備TAM縮減，但 Ins3 機台需求增加：若省略 Ins2，SoIC 後的良品與不良品 OE 都將直接進入 Ins3 受
測，受測量放大為 1÷SoIC 良率倍，Ins3 因受測 OE 增加，所需機台數也將大幅提升。但因省下整段 Ins2 機台，

整體而言 2027/2028 年測試設備新增 TAM 仍將分別減少約 12%/16%，可為客戶省下更多設備投資成本。

省略Ins2推升貼合機需求，2027年貼合機TAM增加約24%
圖26、FAU貼合位置示意圖
資料來源：TSMC、福邦投顧整理預估
FAU為光纖與PIC之間的耦合介面 ，現行有兩種貼合設計： FAU貼合於光引擎側邊 ，負責將光纖與PIC耦合
。現行設計分為兩種：先貼 receptacle再安裝FAU，以及直接將FAU貼合於OE上；前者可能需要兩道貼合製
程，後者僅需一次。
省略Ins2後不良品也須貼合 ，2027年貼合機需求增加約24%：以直接貼合FAU估算，有做Insertion 2時，僅
SoIC後的良品OE需要貼合；若省略Insertion 2，未經篩選的不良品也會一併進入貼合製程，使2027年貼合機
TAM增加約24%。隨SoIC良率提升、不良品減少，兩者差距逐步收斂 ，2028年已大致相當 。這也代表在量
產初期良率仍低時，省略Insertion 2的成本不只是測試，也會反映在貼合機台與貼合材料的額外消耗上。

品；若最終採 receptacle 兩段貼合設計，貼合機需求最高可倍增，萬潤作為現行主要供應商受惠幅度最大。其
他台廠方面，高明鐵、惠特目前均在送樣階段，國際競爭者則包括 MRSI 及港建代理的 ADST。

Document 282: Expected 2027 TMT supply points to a new record

### Page 1

European TMT gross bond supply may reach €150bn in 2027,
driven by tech funding for AI and data centres rather than
traditional telecoms. Strong cash ﬂows and credit quality
support issuance, but disciplined funding will be key to market
absorption
Jan Frederik Slijkerman
AI and data centre investment is expected to be the main driver of TMT bond
issuance in 2027
We predict another year of strong supply for the European TMT
sector. We expect €150bn of gross issuance and €103bn of net

issuance, given €47bn of debt redemptions. The additional
issuance is not coming from the traditional telecom or media
issuers, but from global tech companies.
As we expect the data centre build-out to continue in 2027, issuers
are likely to return to the European investment-grade credit market
to raise part of the required funding. Meanwhile, we do not see a
major reason for traditional sectors to reduce or increase

Telecoms supply
We will break down the main factors aﬀecting debt issuance,
starting with FY27 capex requirements. While we expect capex as a
percentage of sales to decline, aggregate capex is likely to increase

slightly. Companies such as Orange and Vodafone are integrating
recent acquisitions and may need to invest additional capital to
realise merger synergies. We therefore do not expect the lower
capex-to-sales ratio to translate into less issuance. Nevertheless,
BT and KPN are exceptions, as both clearly intend to reduce capex
in FY27.
Low-single-digit EBITDA growth gives companies some capacity to
issue additional debt while maintaining stable leverage. However,

higher interest rates provide an incentive to keep debt levels in
check. We therefore expect the increase in debt issuance to be
driven mainly by M&A. Several debt-funded acquisitions of smaller
networks could generate around €5bn of net issuance. A further
€3bn could come from US operators raising euro-denominated
funding for spectrum payments in 2027.

### Page 3

Technology supply
In 2027, we believe the AI build-out will continue. Tech companies
currently report healthy returns on investment, demand for AI
services continues to outpace supply, and companies want to
maintain their market positions. We therefore expect further capex
growth in 2027, with the large hyperscalers investing close to
US$1tr in ﬁscal 2027.
As in 2026, we expect these companies to ﬁnance their
investments largely from operating cash ﬂow, but also through

debt issuance, with additional funding coming from European
markets. We expect hyperscaler data centre operators to issue at
least €60bn in 2027, somewhat above the approximately €35bn
issued year-to-date in 2026, including issuance by NTT.
In addition to the €60bn of issuance from hyperscaler data centre
operators, we expect €35bn of net supply from other technology
issuers. Companies are likely to pursue acquisitions and raise
additional funding for investment, including semiconductor,

software and payment services companies. Including €8bn from
telecom issuers, this brings total TMT net supply to €103bn. This
would translate into gross supply of approximately €150bn, which,
in our view, is close to the amount the European market may be
able to absorb.
Supply discipline will be important
Companies will need to issue prudently and provide clear guidance
on future funding plans, as spreads may only tighten once the
market no longer expects substantial new supply. Nevertheless,

current spreads look attractive relative to the issuers’ high ratings,
with much of the widening driven by supply and liquidity eﬀects.
Relatively wide CDS spreads also suggest that technical factors,
rather than a material deterioration in credit risk, are weighing on
valuations. This combination of high credit quality and attractive
spreads should attract new money to the investment-grade
market.

Challenge 1: Does debt issuance reduce
credit quality?
First, we expect the hyperscaler data centre operators Amazon,
Microsoft, Alphabet, Meta and Oracle to grow their combined
EBITDA by US$230bn, or 29%, in FY27, to around US$1tr. Moreover,
with the exception of Oracle, these companies are expected to
operate with limited net debt, with Microsoft holding more cash
than debt. The main caveat is their signiﬁcant lease commitments,
many of which are not yet reﬂected in balance-sheet lease

rating agencies will attach greater weight to these liabilities,
particularly if returns on investment start to decline. In our view, this
is not a material concern for 2027 and may never become one,
given the sector’s strong growth and ability to fund a signiﬁcant
part of investment from operating cash ﬂow. Companies also retain
ﬂexibility to scale back future investment that has not yet been
committed if returns start to disappoint.
Challenge 2: Do credit benchmarks

accommodate more supply?
Do European credit markets have the capacity to absorb additional
supply from technology companies? We think the current share of
technology issuers remains modest. Alphabet accounts for only
1.16% of the iBoxx € Non-Financials index, while Amazon and IBM
account for 0.68% and 0.91%, respectively.
In the US, Oracle’s share of more than 2% in the iBoxx $ Domestic
Non-Financials index illustrates that individual technology issuers

can reach materially higher benchmark weights, while several high-

Document 283: Fed builds credibility with hike; one more likely in Dec

Source PDF: all report pdf/Fed builds credibility with hike; one more likely in Dec.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Fed builds credibility with 
hike; one more likely in Dec 
Summary 
Event: The US Federal Reserve hiked its key interest rate by 
25bps to 3.75-4.0%, in a unanimous decision. This marks the 
first hike since 2023, reversing the easing cycle that had 
taken rates down from 4.25% in September 2025 to a trough 
of 3.75% by December 2025. The revised projections 
indicate the Fed expects to raise rates by an additional 25bps 
this year, hold rates in 2027, and cut 25bps in both 2028 and 
2029. It upgraded the long-run rate slightly to 3.2%.  
Market reaction: The US yield curve mildly flattened , while 
the S&P500 index fell 0.5%. The policy rate sensitive US 2 -
year government bond yield jumped 7 bps to 4. 74% as 
markets factored in  three more 25bps hikes by June 2027 . 
The US 10-year yield rose 2bps to 5.02%, its highest since 
2007, while the 30-year yield was little changed. The USD 
index (DXY) rose 0.6%, breaking past its 50- and 100-DMAs. 
Investment strategy: Today’s move realises what we had 
been flagging in recent weeks i.e. the rising risk of a Fed rate 
hike following Fed Chair Warsh’s Jackson Hole speech and 
recent economic data prints, namely: a pick-up in job creation 
and inflation over the summer and a sharp rebound in oil 
prices due to the re-escalation of the Middle East conflict.  
This hike helps the Fed rebuild credibility as an inflation 
fighter. Given the unanimous decision to hike rates this week, 
we believe there will likely be one more hike before the end 
of this year, and one more in H1 2027 as we expect robust 
growth amid strong AI investments and a tight labour market, 
keeping inflation elevated.  That scenario would still be less 
than the three more rate hikes money markets are pricing 
after the latest Fed move. We believe policy rates can return 
to the 4.0 –4.25% range by end-2027, with the possibility of 
cuts in H2 2027  as the impact of tariffs and high oil prices 
start to fade towards the second half of 2027.   
Today’s refreshed Fed policy rate path is mildly bullish for the 
USD index; so we revise up our 3 -month target to 100. We 
also revise up our 3-month 10-year yield target to 5.0-5.25%.  
 The USD rose, while stocks fell after the Fed hiked rates 
and signalled one more rate hike this year; ultra-long 
bonds rose slightly as the Fed rebuild its inflation-
fighting credentials

still sees inflation cooling over the next two years

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 17 September 2026

Backdrop to the rate hike 
In hiking rates, Fed Chair Warsh reaffirmed the inflation 
threat to the US economy. US headline inflation accelerated 
to 0.4% m/m in August and stayed elevated on an annualised 
basis at 3.4% y/y. Energy prices , driven by the Middle East 
conflict, were a primary contributor. Oil prices have extended 
gains since the August inflation report, surging past USD 
100/bbl. Meanwhile, core inflation rose 0.3% m/m in August, 
beating estimates for a 0.2% rise . Also, US producer prices 
jumped 0.4% m/m in August, the most since May, and 5.4%  
y/y, further reinforcing the inflationary backdrop. 
The inflation report follow ed a stronger -than-expected jobs 
report for August. US monthly job creation has surged to an 
average 80,000 this year, from 10,000, tightening a job 
market that already faces a supply crunch from the Trump 
administration’s clampdown against immigration.  The 
combination of an acceleration in job creation and inflation 
over the summer, combined with a rebound in oil prices, 
supported our expectation for a Fed rate hike this week.  
Removing “a dose of accommodation” 
Warsh framed the decision to hike as removing "a dose of 
accommodation", rather than the start of an aggressive rate 
hiking cycle, and we agree with that characterisation. The aim 
is to bring financial and credit conditions more in line with the 
Fed's price stability objectives. To quote Warsh: “Our 
predominant focus is on the price stability side of our 
mandate. The plain fact is that inflation is too high, and has 
been for too long….This  summer’s inflation readings do not 
tell me that underlying trends have meaningfully improved.” 
He added that many categories in the last consumer and 
producer inflation reports are still showing price increases 
above 3% on a six- and 12-month basis.  
The unanimous vote to hike rates, contrasts with a 9 -3 split 
vote in July to hold rates. When combined with the pencilling 
in of one more hike by December, this suggests a majority of 
Fed policymakers have turned more hawkish over the 
summer. In doing so, they have helped rebuild the Fed’s 
inflation-fighting credentials and its independence against 
President Trump’s pressures to cut rates. This explains why 
long-end bond yields were stable after this week’s hike. 
Nevertheless, Warsh provided little guidance on the future 
rate path, as he held what was reportedly the shortest post -
meeting press conference on record for a Fed chair . This is 
consistent with his stated preference for less central bank 
communication. We expect more clarity once the Fed task 
forces submit their reports by year end. 
— Jonathan Liang, CIO for Fixed Income and FX   
Rajat Bhattacharya, Senior Investment Strategist 
 US job creation has accelerated this year after a slump 
last year, tightening a job market already facing a 
potential crunch from an immigration clampdown

### Page 4

◆ The economy remains on the recovery track despite weakness in some
areas
Annualized real GDP growth in the second quarter was +1.1% QoQ, with growth
staying in positive territory for three straight quarters. Although personal
consumption exhibited weakness and capital investment declined, overall growth
was lifted by foreign demand. Exports, particularly of services, increased, and the
drop in imports of Middle Eastern crude oil also helped foreign demand turn positive.

◆ Housing starts are weak
Housing starts surged 18.6% YoY in June. They have continued to rise on a YoY
basis, but the jump was mainly due to a rebound from the slump in the same month
of the previous year. The recovery in the number of starts is actually moderate,
reflecting higher interest rates and soaring construction costs.
◆ Earthquake impact will need to be watched
Damage from the earthquake that occurred in Kumamoto Prefecture at the end of

July is weighing on economic activity in the affected areas. Although moves toward
recovery can be seen, with many companies having already resumed operations,
the impact could become prolonged in the following scenarios:
The first is if damage to capital stock such as factories and roads has been severe.
Some companies have sustained damage to production facilities, making an early
resumption of operations difficult in some cases.
The second is if a downturn in tourism demand occurs. According to some media

reports, there has been an uptick in cancellations of accommodation reservations in
Kumamoto Prefecture and neighboring prefectures since the earthquake. After the
2016 Kumamoto earthquake, the decline in travel demand from Japanese visitors
was short lived, but lower demand from foreign tourists persisted for a long period.
Annualized real GDP growth was +1.1% quarter over quarter (QoQ) in Q2 2026

### Page 5

Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026
The Japan Research Institute, Limited
Exports and inbound demand remain solid despite weakness in some areas
◆ Goods exports are on the verge of picking up
Goods exports are treading water. A sharp rise in memory chip prices is among
factors pushing up export prices, resulting in record high levels of exports in value
terms recently. On the other hand, the increase in volume terms is limited. One

possible reason is that Japan's export competitiveness has declined in fields such as
semiconductors, where global demand is growing.
Looking ahead, exports are expected to pick up. Capital investment by chipmakers
is expanding worldwide, and this should lift exports of semiconductor manufacturing
equipment, an area in which Japan has strength. However, continued caution is
needed regarding the effects of prolonged turmoil in the Middle East. A global

slowdown due to elevated crude oil prices could weigh on exports.
◆ Reluctance to visit among Chinese travelers is weighing on demand for
travel to Japan
Inbound demand is currently soft. The Chinese government's advisory urging its
citizens to refrain from traveling to Japan is having an adverse impact. The number
of Chinese visitors to Japan has fallen to about half the level prior to the advisory.
Meanwhile, the number of tourists from countries other than China remains firm.

Inbound demand is expected to stay robust going forward. Continued increases in
tourists from countries other than China will remain a supporting factor. The
expectation that the yen will remain weak will also act to push up inbound
consumption. However, there are two downside risks going forward:
The first is that the Chinese government keeps the advisory in place for a
prolonged period. When China banned group travel to South Korea in 2017, tourist

numbers stayed low for two to three years.
The second is the impact of higher crude oil prices. Major airlines have embarked
on phased increases in fuel surcharges, which could weigh on inbound demand
during the summer.

### Page 6

Personal consumption is gradually recovering thanks to a favorable income environment
◆ Higher disposable incomes are supporting personal consumption
Personal consumption continues to gradually recover. While consumption of
non-durable goods remains weak, services consumption is firm, particularly in
amusement-related services. In addition, consumption of durable goods has
increased significantly. Temporary factors such as the abolition of the environmental

performance tax on automobiles (since April 2026) and the tightening of
energy-efficiency standards for air conditioners (effective April 2027) have also
contributed to this rise.
A favorable income environment can also be pointed to as a background factor
behind the recovery in personal consumption. YoY real disposable income growth has
generally been positive since mid-2024, and the pace of increase has recently
accelerated. Employee remuneration is rising, partly as a result of agreements made

during the shunto (spring wage negotiations), and net asset income is also increasing
at a faster clip as companies hike dividends in line with stock price rises.
◆ Stock price developments could be a source of concern for personal
consumption
Personal consumption is expected to continue its gradual recovery going forward. In
addition to the favorable income environment and government policy measures to
curb price increases, a recovery in consumer confidence driven by easing tensions in

the Middle East will also provide a tailwind. Recently, the Consumer Confidence Index
has been showing signs of bottoming out.
However, a reverse wealth effect stemming from falling stock prices is a potential
cause for concern. The Nikkei Stock Average, which reached a new high in June, has
been struggling to push higher since the start of July. Estimates suggest the wealth
effect is concentrated among elderly households, but in recent years working-age

households have also increased their holdings of equity-related assets, and the
positive wealth effect for them may have supported consumption to some extent. For
this reason, if stock prices were to fall significantly, there is a danger of downward
pressure on consumption across a wide range of households.

### Page 7

セン
To
◆ The unemployment rate has settled in the mid-2% range
While the employment situation is generally favorable, weakness can be seen in
some areas. The unemployment rate has remained in the mid-2% range since 2022.
The number of employed persons in non-manufacturing, where business conditions
are favorable, is holding firm, while in manufacturing the number remains at a level
below that seen before the COVID-19 pandemic.
In the background to the drop in manufacturing employment is a slump in

production activity. Manufacturing output has been shrinking due to the effects of
U.S. tariff policy, soaring raw material prices, and a decline in the international
competitiveness of Japanese firms. As a result, pressure to reduce headcounts has
been building. Even in non-manufacturing, some industries closely linked to
manufacturing, such as transportation and wholesale/retail, are also exhibiting
weakness in labor demand.
◆ The employment situation could remain fragile in the near term

Looking ahead at the employment situation in the short term, a sense of
stagnation may persist. The contraction in output in the manufacturing sector will
continue to weigh on labor demand. Although production activity has been brisker
recently, particularly in semiconductor-related industries, the employment-creation
capacity of such industries is small. The employment-inducement coefficient per unit
of new demand tends to be lower than in other sectors, meaning that the industries

lack the strength to serve as drivers of employment.
However, in the medium to long term, the unemployment rate is expected to see a
gradual decline. Demand for labor is projected to stay solid, especially in
face-to-face services such as medical/nursing care and accommodation and
eating/drinking services, but the number of people seeking employment from the
non-labor force population has declined significantly due to the expansion in elderly
and female workforce participation. With limited room for expansion in the labor

supply, the job market is expected to tighten further.

The government is curbing inflation
◆ Policy factors are pushing down the inflation rate
In June, core inflation (all items, less fresh food) was 1.6%, staying under 2%
for the fifth consecutive month. Food price inflation has slowed, and a major

reason for this is a fallback in rice prices, which soared last year. Gasoline
subsidies are also continuing to curtail energy price increases.
Inflation looks set to hover in the 2% range in the second half of 2026. Although
energy subsidies and other government measures will soften inflationary pressure
to some extent, with imported inflationary pressure building, prices are expected
to be pushed up as higher costs are passed through to selling prices. From

FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items will
serve to keep prices down, and inflation is expected to drop into the 1% range.
◆ The Bank of Japan (BOJ) will keep its policy rate unchanged
At its July monetary policy meeting, the BOJ decided to keep its policy rate
unchanged.
Long-term interest rates remain high. This reflects concerns about inflation
resulting from the increase in oil prices and expectations of an expansion in fiscal

expenditure.
The BOJ is expected to step up the pace of interest rate hikes while closely
monitoring the impact of higher rates on the real economy and financial/capital
markets. The next rate hike is expected to be in September.
Long-term interest rates look likely to rise gradually, reflecting heightened
anticipation of rate hikes by the BOJ and increased fiscal spending by the
government. If jitters about a deterioration in fiscal discipline resulting from such

measures as energy subsidies or the consumption tax cut take hold, there is also
a risk that long-term interest rates will rise.

◆ Surging crude oil prices have become a source of upward pressure on
prices
With crude oil prices having spiked against the backdrop of the worsening situation
in the Middle East, corporate input costs have risen sharply, and pressure for price
pass-through is intensifying. Looking at the Final Demand-Intermediate Demand
price indexes, the margin of price increases at the upstream stage far exceeds that at
the downstream stage, and price pass-through pressure is also substantial at the

downstream stage.
According to estimates based on past data, the impact of rising oil prices involves
a time lag. Specifically, the push-up effect on the consumer price inflation rate
increases for around 10 months and continues to exert influence for a long period
thereafter.
◆ Price pass-through willingness is also stronger than before
Notably, food product manufacturers are planning to raise prices for a huge
number of items during the summer and beyond. According to Teikoku Databank, the

number of items slated for price increases as of the end of July, when it conducted its
latest survey, was higher than in the May and June surveys.
If government subsidies for electricity/gas bills and gasoline prices are scaled back
or ended, both energy and food prices will increase concurrently, and the consumer
price inflation rate may rise again.
Attention also needs to be paid to the possibility that price pass-through driven by
elevated oil prices will become prolonged. In the 2020s, the rate of price increases in

transactions between companies has been higher than in the 2010s, and firms'
medium-term outlooks for selling prices have also shifted upward. Events that trigger
cost increases, such as heightened geopolitical tensions, have been occurring
repeatedly, and companies may be more willing to pass through higher costs to
prices than they were before.

### Page 10

The economy is expected to continue to recover, but downside risks are present
◆ The economy is projected to stay on the recovery track
The Japanese economy should continue its gradual recovery. WTI crude oil
futures prices are expected to fall moderately going forward.
In the corporate sector, government support for investment is providing a tailwind,
and demand for investment for labor saving and digital transformation remains
robust. In addition, rising global demand for AI-related goods should support exports.

Supply chain disruptions resulting from the deterioration in the Middle East situation
will begin to ease, and corporate production activity is expected to gradually recover.
In the household sector, inflationary pressure is expected to strengthen from the
summer onward due to higher crude oil prices. In response, the government will
probably ease the inflationary pressure through measures such as energy subsidies.
While the pace of increase in personal consumption is likely to slow somewhat,

consumption is expected to remain resilient amid continued high wage growth. From
FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items should also
support personal consumption.
◆ The Middle East situation and rising interest rates constitute risks
Real GDP growth is projected to remain solid, registering +0.8% in FY2026 and
+0.9% in FY2027.
However, if the situation in the Middle East worsens again or becomes prolonged,
there is a risk that downward pressure on the economy will intensify, mainly through

three channels: 1) weaker exports, 2) raw material supply constraints, and 3) rising
prices. In addition, if market concerns about the fiscal stance of the Takaichi
government grow, the yen could weaken further, and long-term interest rates could
rise, potentially forcing the scaling-back or scrapping of expansionary policies such
as investment support measures, energy subsidies, and lower consumption tax.
Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026

The Japan Research Institute, Limited- ８ -

Document 327: Next Generation EU_ The programme is coming to an end, but its effects on economic activity will endure (2026-09-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Next Generation EU_ The programme is coming to an end, but its effects on economic activity will endure (2026-09-23).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-23
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

23 September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
NEXT GENERATION EU: THE PROGRAMME IS COMING TO AN END, BUT ITS EFFECTS ON ECONOMIC ACTIVITY WILL
ENDURE
Guillaume Derrien
A pillar of the NextGenerationEU programme, the Recovery and Resilience Facility (RRF) will expire on December 31, 2026. As of the
end of August 2026, slightly more than three-quarters of the total budget had been disbursed (EUR 440 billion out of EUR 573 billion).
Disbursing the remaining quarter (EUR 133 billion) by the end of the year will require a significant increase compared with the histo-

rical pace (EUR 90 billion per year). The goal of channeling these funds toward productive investment appears to have been achieved.
Tangible results for investment in Europe
According to Eurostat, public spending co-financed by the RRF totaled EUR 310 billion between 2020 and 2025. Nearly three-quarters (74%)
consisted of capital expenditures, including EUR 106 billion in direct public investment and EUR 122 billion in capital transfers to the private

sector (such as grants). This spending supported investment by businesses and households.
The list of major programmes funded by the RFF, published by the European Commission 1, shows that, within this scope, nearly two-thirds of
the funding went to transportation (primarily rail), energy infrastructure, and digital infrastructure (see Chart 1).
Another notable point: capital expenditures co-financed by the RRF increased significantly, reaching 0.4% of GDP for the European Union as a

whole in 2025 (see Chart 2). There are significant disparities among Member States, primarily due to differences in the amounts allocated to
them. Croatia (1.9%), Greece (1.2%), Italy (1%), and Spain (0.7%) received significant support, in contrast to France and Germany (0.1%). These
figures are consistent with the IMF’s estimate that the RRF added approximately 0.3 percentage points (pp) to EU growth in 2025 (more than
1 pp in Greece and Croatia, around 0.8 pp in Italy, and 0.5 pp in Spain) 2. Furthermore, as the first major joint European borrowing initiative, the

RRF made it possible to finance investment without increasing national budget deficits, giving several countries (Spain, Italy, Greece, Portugal)
valuable breathing room to consolidate their public finances.
1 Recovery and Resilience Scoreboard
2 The EU’s Recovery and Resilience Facility: Experience and Lessons for the Next Multiannual Financial Framework, WP/26/114, June 2026
SOURCE: EUROPEAN COMMISSION, Recovery and Resilience Scorecard

What impact on EU’s economic activity can we expect once this programme ends?
At first glance, effects will be limited. The end of the programme is expected to weigh on both public and private investment. However, the
strengthening of economic activity that we anticipate (see details in our latest Ecoperspectives – Q3 2026) rests primarily on German public

investment programmes and structural drivers (investment in AI, defence spending).
The ECB makes the same point in its September macroeconomic projections 3. It emphasises that the end of the RRF is expected to weigh
on public investment in 2027, while it anticipates an acceleration in business investment. Furthermore, RRF funds are being absorbed on
the ground only gradually. Since most disbursements are concentrated toward the end of the programme, the effects of the RRF will extend

beyond 2026.
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

Document 346: Sales Trader Commentary_ TAIEX 49k Wobble - July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa Rally_ _ GSTW

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sales Trader Commentary_ TAIEX 49k Wobble - July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa Rally_ _ GSTW.pdf

### Page 1

Sales Trader Commentary: TAIEX 49k Wobble -
July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa
Rally? | GSTW
13 October 2026 | 4:11 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Index lost the 49k points threshold and saw the biggest decline on a point basis since
the end of July. Market seemed to lose the steam to break through 50k points after
the past choppy sessions. Is this July de ja vu or some healthy pullback? Foreigners

and domestic investors seem to share different narrative. Check out below for our
investor feedback.
Key Investor Feedback and Observations
Positioning. we saw meaningful outflows today from all kinds of investors today,
again focusing in ex-TSMC large caps. Today foreigners net sold US$5.2bn today,
the second largest in the history (simultaneously drove TWD weakness over 32.23
today). Domestic investors, especially retail, on the other hand, anchored the

market with the record high margin loan balance (as of 10/12/2026). Local funds
remained buyer as well mainly thanks to ETFs inflow.
Macro. CPI headline brought uncertainty and forced global investors to turn
cautious, which was exacerbated by high yields and crude. For Taiwan, AI trade is
relatively resilient to the Middle Eastern tension and high energy prices. However,
at some point, it is going to have a real economic impact either through Fed or

some other macro data.
Sentiment. Surprisingly, people are still bullish in Taiwan, though they also
acknowledge some choppy trading sessions ahead. Local investor feedback is not
calling for capitulation; in fact, most believe this is just another "two-steps forward
one-step backward" cadence towards another record high. Nonetheless, some
retail start taking chips off the table. This echoes our observation among the
foreign investors. Yes, the AI fundamental future is all sunshine, but as we head into

the end of year, it is natural for them to protect their great fun thus far this year
instead of adding on immediate risk. I'd imagine the next few weeks are going to be
full of 1) rapid sector rotation; 2) weak or unsustainable momentum before Santa
comes into town after the midterm election for our year-end rally Christmas gift.
TSMC Earnings and Others. We know the numbers are going to be good, and we
also know people are going to sell-on-facts after TSMC earnings call; recall most

recent one happened right after the meeting that scarred a lot of investors since.
Perhaps this time people started trimming ahead (and maybe too early?), which
provides some window for a quick rebound into small and mid cap. OCP summit is
happening right now, and TPCA Show will start next week. Watch out for single
stock movement mentioned in the conferences.
10/13/26, 4:13 PM Sales Trader Commentary: TAIEX 49k Wobble - July De Ja Vu, or Just Pausing Before the Santa Rally? | GSTW

Sector. 1) ABF/CCL is still the crowded long sectors for both foreign and domestic
investor, but echoing on above points, investors need more catalysts to add on
risks or drive the re-ratings. 2) Power is another big issues that data centers need
to resolve, power solutions and fuel cells name are also key focus in Taiwan. 3) Let's
not forget about OSATs. We need more equipment and testing capacity to suffice
enlarging demand from both ASIC and GPU.

Key Stocks I Follow: Unimicron, Kinsus, NYPCB, ZDT, EMC, TUC, Delta, Lite-On,
Kaori, ASE, KYEC, All Ring
Risk. Upside looks thin in the short term. Good earnings are not enough - look at
Nanya Tech and Memory sector today. Chasing momentum now may get hurt. I also
smelled some short unwind today (i.e. funding short names outperformance such
as Realtek, Novatek, Shipper, etc) - signs of de-leveraging. Directionless is how I
would describe the past few days, but maybe it's time to find some value Buy?

10282, Attn: Prospectus Department. ETFs are redeemable only in Creation Unit size aggregations and may not be individually
redeemed; are redeemable only through Authorized Participants; and are redeemable on an "in-kind" basis. The public trading price of a

redeemable lot of the ETFs may be different from its net asset value. These ETFs can trade at a discount or premium to the net asset
value. There is always a fundamental risk of declining stock prices, which can cause losses to your investment. Some leveraged and
inverse ETFs and ETNs "reset" daily, meaning that they are designed to achieve their stated objectives on a daily basis. They will therefore
realize each day a loss or a gain which is a multiple (for leveraged ETFs and ETNs) or the inverse (for inverse ETFs and ETNs) of the

performance on that day of their underlying index or benchmark. For this reason, if they are held for a period longer than one day, their
performance over such longer periods of time can differ significantly from the stated multiple of the performance (or the inverse of the
performance) of their underlying index or benchmark during the same period of time. This effect can be magnified in volatile markets.
Prior to entering into a transaction in leveraged or inverse ETFs, you should be aware of the general risks associated with such

transactions. You should not enter into leveraged or inverse ETFs transactions unless you understand the nature and extent of your risk
exposure. You should also be satisfied that the leveraged or inverse ETFs transaction is appropriate for you in light of your circumstances
and financial condition. ETFs and ETNs that are linked to commodity futures do not offer direct exposure to the commodity's spot price,
and may perform differently than the spot price for the commodity itself. Performance differential can be magnified if a specific

condition persists in the market for a commodity that creates a disparity between near-term future prices and long-term future prices
and may lead to unexpected performance results. Other factors, such as roll yield, transaction costs, management fees, and taxes may
cause deviation in performance between the spot price of a commodity and commodity futures. You should not assume that an ETF or
ETN that is linked to commodity futures will provide an effective hedge because of a negative correlation with equities or other asset

swaps, and other derivatives as well as non-investment-grade securities - give rise to substantial risk and are not available to nor suitable
for all investors. If you have any questions about whether you are eligible to enter into these transactions with Goldman Sachs, please
contact your sales representative. Foreign-currency-denominated securities are subject to fluctuations in exchange rates that could have
an adverse effect on the value or price of, or income derived from, the investment. In addition, investors in securities such as ADRs, the

values of which are influenced by foreign currencies, effectively assume currency risk.

At a Glance: Geopolitical events to watch in October 2026
Our monthly Look-Ahead covers a non-exhaustive list of key events, issues and themes to watch in the month ahead. The below map intends to serve as an
executive summary, providing a list of the issues at a glance. These are covered in more detail in subsequent slides. For an overview of key events between

October and December 2026, see our latest 90-day Geopolitical Risk Radar.

### Page 3

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
Diplomacy appears firmly back in play. The past month saw continued fighting and renewed diplomacy.
Reporting suggests that the US is prepared to extend troop deployments in the Middle East to 2027. Iran
targeted both US forces in the region and some tankers moving through the Strait, but has been unable to
prevent a significant rebound in crude flows through Hormuz. On 22 SEP, talks were mediated by Qatar,

Pakistan and Egypt, and on 24 SEP, Iran presented the US with a seven-day roadmap to a ceasefire leading
to a phased reopening of the Strait of Hormuz and a return to nuclear negotiations, conditional on a US
lifting of its blockade of Iran, sanctions relief and broader cessation of hostilities. The Trump
Administration rejected the proposal, but negotiations continue.
To watch will be whether October is the month the war transitions into a negotiated settlement, which

may hinge on whether Iran is willing to make some concessions on sanctions relief and the nuclear
question, and whether Trump is keen to demonstrate that the war has produced a political and economic
result in the final weeks before the 3 NOV midterm elections. It is plausible that the current diplomatic
channel could produce a temporary de-escalation mechanism as long as there is pathway around the
broader nuclear issue.
Both sides will reconvene for trade talks in Beijing on 8-9 October, which the EU views as a deadline for

“tangible results”. Brussels remains focused on its rising trade deficit with China – which has reached a
record €1 billion per day – and the impact of Chinese goods on European industry. The bloc has vowed to
take measures if talks fail.
While both sides could achieve progress in talks, a lasting breakthrough appears improbable, setting the
stage for more trade tensions into 2027. New EU trade investigations, curbs on Chinese imports, and EU-
level instruments intended to address alleged Chinese overcapacity, are possible outcomes, to which

Beijing has stated it would respond.
China-EU trade talks could also impact China-US relations. Should talks with Brussels fall short of averting
increased European trade pressure, we assess that Beijing may further prioritise near-term US stability. See
Rules of Engagement: Mapping the Next Phase of China-US Ties for more.
In the spotlight for October 2026: US-Iran war and EU-China relations
US-Iran war
EU-China trade relations
Strait of Hormuz total liquids exports (mmb/d)

### Page 4

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
Latvia will elect all 100 members of the 15th Saeima. Latvia, which borders Russia and Belarus, has been
among the most vocal supporters of Ukraine. The next government will set the country’s course on
defence spending, Ukraine aid, sanctions enforcement and border security. The field is highly fragmented
with the governing parties lacking a majority in the polls. The fragmentation matters as a weakened and

multi-party scramble could mean a slower, more unstable coalition.
Brazil’s first electoral round is on 4 OCT, with a runoff on 25 OCT. This is a critical election as it will
decide whether Latin America’s biggest economy continues President Lula’s state-led social spending or
pivots to a more fiscally-orthodox agenda under Bolsonaro. Polls have gone from a Lula lead in the spring
to a statistical dead heat between Lula and Bolsonaro. A Brazilian Supreme Court crisis – which has

implicated Supreme Court Justices and politicians on both sides – is weighing on the race. Congressional
and gubernatorial races will also impact the governability of the country.
This election will also shape whether the country hedges between Washington and Beijing under Lula or
tilts toward closer US trade and security alignment under Bolsonaro with potential implications for
tariffs, BRICS+ cohesion, and Latin America’s broader positioning between the two powers.

The meetings will take place in Bangkok, bringing finance ministers, central bankers, policy-makers and
business leaders together. The agenda is expected to centre on global growth, financial stability, debt,
trade and investment, development finance and the impact of AI on jobs and productivity, with particular
attention given to mobilizing private capital. The key watchpoint will be the outlook for global growth
and inflation, debt vulnerabilities, the future of the international financial architecture and how policy-

makers respond to increasing fragmentation in trade and capital flows.
Key events, issues and themes to watch in October 2026
Listed in chronological order
General election in Brazil (4 October)
Source: Waranont (Joe) on Unsplash
Latvian elections (3 October)
Annual meetings of the International Monetary Fund and World Bank (12-18 October)

### Page 5

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
Leaders are expected to focus heavily on Ukraine, European security and defence, competitiveness and
the EU’s economic position in a more contested global environment. They will also take forward
negotiations on the 2028-2034 EU long-term budget. They set themselves a soft deadline in June to get
the budget broadly locked down because missing it pushes ratification into 2027 and risks a funding gap

for beneficiaries from JAN 2028. To watch will be a genuine breakthrough on the multiannual framework
or more likely what “substantial” progress may look like. Ukraine funding is the other pressure point. This
meeting will be important for understanding the direction of European defence spending and industrial
policy, the next phase of budget negotiations and the EU response to growing external economic
pressure and strategic dependencies.
The Canadian province of Alberta will hold a referendum, which includes a question about the province’s

future within Canada. This is not an independence vote itself but asking voters to choose between
remaining a Canadian province and asking the Alberta government to begin the constitutional process
required for a future binding referendum on separation. Current polling suggests “remain” is comfortably
ahead. The significance is around potential renewed uncertainty around Canada’s federal cohesion,
energy policy, fiscal transfers and Alberta's relationship with Ottawa and Washington. Even a modest

agreement to begin a process towards secession would likely keep alive equalization grievances as a
structural fault line in Canadian federalism feeding into political risk.
.
The event’s theme focuses on “Advancing Alliances in a New Era.” It will tackle how governments and
businesses respond to a world where geopolitics, economic security, supply chains and technology are
increasingly intertwined. There is likely to be a strong focus on the future of the transatlantic

relationship, including how Europe adapts to a more demanding security environment. There will also be
discussions of tariffs, supply chain resilience, critical capabilities and the weaponization of economic
interdependence. It will align with Chancellor Merz’s efforts to ensure Europe actively shapes outcomes
around strategic autonomy and reducing China exposure in manufacturing.
Key events, issues and themes to watch in October 2026
Listed in chronological order

Alberta independence referendum (19 October)
Source: Bernd Dittrich on Unsplash
European Council meeting (15-16 October)
Berlin Global Dialogue (20-22 October)
4

### Page 6

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
The second summit will take place in Saudi Arabi, bringing EU and Gulf leaders together. The agenda is
expected to focus on regional security and the Middle East, energy and economic security, trade and
investment, infrastructure, technology and broader strategic cooperation. The security dimension will be
particularly salient given the current crisis in the region. Economically, the relationship is substantial: EU-

GCC trade reached €165.6bn in 2025.
To watch will be movement on the long-stalled EU-GCC free trade talks and visa-waiver requests from
Qatar and Saudi Arabia in addition to the extent of the focus on Gaza reconstruction, Iran and regional
security get relative to the economic agenda.
GCC states have consistently used these summits to press for clearer EU positioning on Middle East
conflicts in exchange for economic cooperation. The summit comes amid at a critical juncture where

Europe is recalibrating its external partnerships.
The Central Committee of the Communist Party of China will hold its Fifth Plenary Session in Beijing.
This will be one of the most important events on the country’s political calendar ahead of the 21st Party
Congress in late 2027.
The Plenum will focus on Party governance, and investors will scrutinise signals regarding leadership
changes and personnel reshuffles, institutional reforms, and political priorities for the next Congress.

While fifth plenums historically focus on economic planning, the development guidelines for the 15th
Five-Year Plan (2026–2030) were already adopted during the Fourth Plenum in OCT 2025, allowing the
upcoming session to focus on personnel and governance matters.
Key events, issues and themes to watch in October 2026
Listed in chronological order
Summit between the EU and the Gulf Cooperation Council (24 October)
Source: istockphoto
Fifth Plenum of China’s Central Committee (26-29 October)

5

### Page 7

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
Elections will be held for the 26th Knesset, the first since the October 7th attacks and wars in Gaza,
Lebanon and Iran. This is the first Knesset since 1988 to actually complete a full four-year term without
early dissolution. Benjamim Netanyahu remains the dominant single figure but faces a fragmented field:
Naftali Bennett’s new “Together” party is polling as the main rival bloc, alongside Gadi Eisenkot’s Yashar,

Benny Gantz’s Blue and White, and the further right Ben Gvir and Smotrich factions.
Israel’s proportional system means the election is effectively about coalition formation. Key faultlines to
watch: the ultra-Orthodox draft-exemption fight, how the war’s legacy and hostage/reconstruction issues
in Gaza factor into campaign messaging, and whether a Netanyahu-led bloc can reassemble a working
majority or whether the Bennett/Eisenkot-style “anyone but Bibi” coalitions gain enough traction. The

outcome will shape Israel’s posture on critical regional issues, including Gaza reconstruction, Iran policy
and West Bank settlement activity.
This summit is shaping up as a particularly important juncture against a more ambitious push to redefine
the relationship. During Mark Carney’s SEP visit to Strasbourg, he called for a comprehensive strategic
alliance with Europe. European Commission President von der Leyen went further, proposing that Canada

become the EU’s first “associate member”.
The agenda is expected to centre on trade and economic security, defense, critical minerals, energy,
digital cooperation and supply-chain resilience, building on the EU-Canada security and Defense
Partnership in the EU’s SAFE defense initiative. The summit will be watched for concrete progress on a
digital trade agreement and broader economic cooperation.
Key events, issues and themes to watch in October 2026

Listed in chronological order
Source: istockphoto
EU-Canada Summit (29-30 October)
Legislative election in Israel (27 October)

### Page 8

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics | October 2026
Deutsche Bank
Research Institute
The foreign ministers’ meeting comes ahead of the December Leaders’ Summit in Miami under the US’
chairmanship of the G20 this year. Trump’s G20 year has organized around four expert tracks – trade,
innovation, energy and deregulation. The session is framed around economic prosperity, energy security
and technological innovation, a much more transactional focus than previous foreign ministerials, which

typically focused on geopolitical crisis management.
It will be a test of whether the G20 can sustain cooperation amid intensifying geopolitical fragmentation.
In addition, Russia has confirmed its intent to actively participate in the meeting. This would further
normalize Russia’s multilateral engagement. For Europe and other partners, a key question will be the
extent to which the G20 can be a forum for broader international cooperation.
Key events, issues and themes to watch in October 2026

Listed in chronological order
G20 Foreign Ministers’ Meeting (30-31 October)
Source: Lance Asper on Unsplash

產業更新 – 半導體測試產業 
Insertion 4 為 CPO 量產化的「最後一公里」 
What’s New – CPO 測試流程逐步走向「系統級出貨驗證」 
CPO 測試流程逐步從「單體功能驗證」走向「系統級出貨驗證」 ，測試對象由 PIC／EIC 等單一
元件，逐步擴展至  Optical Engine ，最終進入完整  CPO 系統的功能、效能與可靠度驗證。
Insertion 1–3 的核心目的，是在 CPO 整合程度與元件價值逐步提高的過程中，盡可能提早攔
截不良品，為後續 Insertion 4 的系統級出貨驗證建立良率基礎。Insertion 4 之所以重要，在於
它是 CPO 從「單體功能驗證」走向「系統級出貨驗證」的最後一道關鍵測試關卡，與前段測試
目的大不相同。 
Trends/Outlook – In 4 競爭關鍵轉向完整光電測試系統整合 
Insertion 4 的測試重點，在於完成 ASIC 與 Optical Engine 整合後，驗證整體 CPO 的光電訊
號傳輸是否符合規格。Insertion 4 必須同時確認兩條關鍵路徑：1) ASIC 與 Optical Engine 之間
的高速電性互聯 。2) Optical Engine 與 FA／FAU 光纖介面之間的光訊號發射 （Tx） 與接收 （Rx）。
在測試架構上，In4 可依光學與電性測試的整合程度，分為「光電分開測」 與 「光電同測」 。光電
分開測較容易沿用既有的半導體與光學測試設備 ， 但較難在相同條件下直接驗證完整的端到端光
電訊號鏈路；光電同測則是在同一測試環境中，同時建立高速電性與光學介面， 可更完整掌握 
Tx/Rx、BER、訊號品質以及溫度變化對整體系統的影響。 
Investment Summary 
綜觀 Insertion 4 供應鏈，海內外廠商皆有參與。Advantest 與 Teradyne 皆已將 CPO 最終封
裝測試納入光電測試布局。Advantest （6857 JP） 主要以既有 V93000 ATE 平台為核心建立
整合式測試架構；Teradyne（TER US）的 Photon 100 明確定位為矽光與 CPO 的光電自動
化量產測試平台。台廠方面，致茂（2360 TT）憑藉既有光學對位與量測能力，先切入 In4o 領
域，佈局涵蓋自動光學連接、校正清潔與插入損耗量測，將受惠於CPO 封裝後光學測試需求持
續增加；鴻勁 （7769 TT） 則聚焦 Handler 與溫控系統 （ATC），In4 光電同測設備將從 FY26 進
入量產型驗證階段，FY27 將持續隨客戶產品開發進度推進，雖 FY26 CPO 相關營收貢獻仍有
限，但將成為中長期成長動能，研究部預期鴻勁於高階 ATC Handler具領先市場地位，將能受惠
於後段測試對精密操作、溫控及自動化整合需求提升。

Insertion4 為 CPO 量產化的「最後一公里」 
CPO 測試流程逐步從「單體功能驗證」走向「系統級出貨驗證」 
CPO 測試流程逐步從 「單體功能驗證」 走向 「系統級出貨驗證」 ，測試對象由 PIC／EIC 等單一元
件，逐步擴展至 Optical Engine，最終進入完整 CPO 系統的功能、效能與可靠度驗證。Insertion 
4 之所以重要，在於它是 CPO 從 「單體功能驗證」 走向 「系統級出貨驗證」 的最後一道關鍵測試
關卡，與前段測試目的大不相同。 
在進入 Insertion 4 系統級測試之前，CPO 前段主要透過 Insertion 1–3 逐步篩選不良元件並確認
光電整合品質。Insertion 1 主要針對尚未整合的 PIC 與 EIC 進行晶圓／裸晶層級測試，分別確
認光學與電性功能是否符合規格，以篩選 Known Good Die （KGD） ，避免不良晶粒進入後續高成
本封裝 。Insertion 2 則發生在 PIC 與 EIC 完成鍵合後 ， 測試重點由單一元件轉向光電整合功能 ，
透過電性探針與光學耦合，同步驗證電轉光（E/O）與光轉電（O/E）訊號是否正常，並檢查鍵合
後的高速訊號與光學性能 。Insertion 3 則進一步來到 Optical Engine 階段 ， 在晶片切割並完成 FAU
／光纖等光學介面組裝後 ， 針對完整光引擎進行 Tx/Rx、 光功率 、BER、 多通道與高速介面等測試 ，
篩選出 Known Good Optical Engine（KGOE） 。因此，Insertion 1–3 的核心目的，是在 CPO 整合程
度與元件價值逐步提高的過程中，盡可能提早攔截不良品，為後續  Insertion 4 的系統級出貨驗
證建立良率基礎。

Advantest、Teradyne、鴻勁、致茂 
資料來源：國泰證期研究部整理

Insertion 4 競爭關鍵逐漸由單一設備性能，轉向完整光電測試系統的整合與自動化能力 
Insertion 4 的測試重點，在於完成 ASIC 與 Optical Engine 整合後，驗證整體 CPO 的光電訊號傳輸
是否符合規格。相較於前段測試著重於個別元件或 Optical Engine，Insertion 4 必須同時確認兩條
關鍵路徑：1) ASIC 與 Optical Engine 之間的高速電性互聯。2) Optical Engine 與 FA／FAU 光纖介
面之間的光訊號發射（Tx）與接收（Rx）。在 Tx 路徑中，需確認 ASIC 輸出的高速電訊號能否經
由 EIC 與 PIC 正確轉換為光訊號並透過光纖送出 ；Rx 路徑則需驗證外部光訊號能否經由光偵測
器與 EIC 轉換回電訊號，再正確傳送至 ASIC。隨著 CPO 傳輸速率與通道數持續提升，測試過程

### Page 3

投資 顧問 
產業報告

2026/10/13

3 
還需進一步考量 BER、訊號完整性、通道間干擾、功耗及溫度變化等因素，使 I4 從單一元件測
試提升至完整光電鏈路的系統級驗證。 
在測試架構上，I4 可依光學與電性測試的整合程度，分為「光電分開測」與「光電同測」 。光電
分開測是將電性與光學測試安排於不同測試步驟或測試站 ， 較容易沿用既有的半導體與光學測試
設備，但較難在相同條件下直接驗證完整的端到端光電訊號鏈路；光電同測則是在同一測試環境
中，同時建立高速電性與光學介面，直接驗證從 ASIC、Optical Engine 到 Fiber 的訊號傳輸，可
更完整掌握 Tx/Rx、BER、訊號品質以及溫度變化對整體系統的影響。 
因此，若 In4 朝光電同測發展，其技術門檻將不再只是  Tester 本身的測試能力，而是如何將  
Tester、Handler、Test Interface、Optical Interface 與 Thermal Control 整合至同一套量產測試平台 。
在兼顧測試精度的同時，設備還必須維持穩定的光學耦合、高速電性接觸與溫度控制，並縮短測
試時間 、 提高平行測試能力， 以滿足量產所需的 throughput。這使 In4 的競爭關鍵逐漸由單一設
備性能，轉向完整光電測試系統的整合與自動化能力。

競爭重點延伸至光學連接自動化、Handler／Interface 整合 
綜觀 Insertion 4 供應鏈，海內外廠商皆有參與。Advantest 與 Teradyne 皆已將 CPO 最終封裝測
試納入光電測試布局。Advantest 主要以既有 V93000 ATE 平台為核心，透過與光學探針、FAU、
Handler 與 Test Interface 生態系夥伴合作，建立可涵蓋  wafer、die 到最終 NPO／CPO package 
test 的整合式測試架構；Teradyne 的 Photon 100 明確定位為矽光與 CPO 的光電自動化量產測
試平台，將光學與電性量測整合進既有 UltraFLEXplus ATE，並可支援 wafer、Optical Engine 以及 
co-packaged module 等不同 insertion。整體而言，兩家公司在 I4 的競爭方向，都是從傳統「電
性 Tester」走向「光電整合測試單元」 ，隨 CPO 進入量產，兩家的競爭重點預期將不只在 Tester 
規格，而會進一步延伸至光學連接自動化、 Handler／Interface 整合、平行測試能力與整體  
throughput。 
台廠方面，致茂（2360 TT）憑藉既有光學對位與量測能力，先切入 In4o 領域，佈局涵蓋自動光
學連接、校正清潔與插入損耗量測，佈局領域完善，將受惠於 CPO 封裝後光學測試需求持續增
加；鴻勁（7769 TT）則聚焦 Handler 與溫控系統（ATC），In4 光電同測設備將從 FY26 進入量產型
驗證階段，FY27 將持續隨客戶產品開發進度推進，雖 FY26 CPO 相關營收貢獻仍有限，但將成為
中長期成長動能，研究部預期鴻勁於高階 ATC Handler 具領先市場地位，將能受惠於後段測試對
精密操作、溫控及自動化整合需求提升。

弘塑 3131 TT  ( 電子上游–設備 ) 
 ) 
訂單能見度無虞 
投資與評價 
1. 弘塑 2Q26 毛利率僅達 38.9%， 低於我們原先及市場預期的40%水準，
主要受到添鴻產品結構轉差及新廠折舊攤提影響。 
2. 3Q26 受惠於 CoWoS 等機台認列數成長及強勁拉貨需求，營收達
22.21 億元；研究部預估 EPS 為 15.86 元。 
3. FY26 全年出機量預估維持在 220 至 240 台，展望至 FY27/FY28，
FY27 設備出貨量預估將年增 50%，FY28 亦有機會再成長 50%。 
4. 研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 60.66 元/96.33 元，維持弘塑買進投
資建議，目標價 2,500 元，以 26x FY27 獲利預估。 
重點更新 
至 FY28 出貨量能將持續成長 
研究部認為弘塑設備出貨展望能見度將能延伸至 FY28。 由於晶圓廠 、OSAT、
記憶體客戶皆對先進封裝設備需求相當迫切，設備交期已長達 10 至 12 個
月，惟受限於短期人力供應與廠房籌備進度， FY26 全年出機量預估維持在 
220 至 240 台。展望至 FY27/FY28，FY27 設備出貨量預估將年增 50%，
FY28 亦有機會再成長 50%。 
添鴻化學品業務將於 1Q27 逐漸看見轉機 
隨 OSAT 客戶於今年 Q2/Q3 進入裝機密集期，新產線仍須經測試與量產爬
坡，相關的化學品需求通常會在設備交機後約 3~4 個月逐步提升，因此短期
營收仍受新廠折舊與稼動率偏低影響，預期添鴻的整體稼動率與毛利率有望
在 1Q27 逐漸擺脫谷底，迎來較為明顯的營運轉機。 
訂單能見度無虞 
展望 2026 年至 2028 年 ， 在各先進封裝平台強勁需求下 ， 設備產能已告全
滿，交期長達 10 至 12 個月，訂單能見度直達 2H27。就產品組合來看，
設備業務高階產品比重將逐漸提升，預估至 FY27 SoIC /CPO 等營收占比將
提升至 10~15%，添鴻化學品也將受惠於 OSAT 客戶逐漸提升拉貨動能，整
體營收與獲利成長動能明確。研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 60.66 元
/96.33 元(YoY＋33.5%/+58.8%)。弘塑目前交易於 35/22 倍之 2026/2027 
年獲利預估。研究部維持買進投資建議，目標價 2,500 元，以 26x FY27 獲
利預估。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
3Q26 受惠於機台認列數成長，營收呈現季增 
弘塑 2Q26 營收 17.27 億元 (QoQ +8.2% / YoY +5.9%)，符合我們原先預估的 16.88 億元；毛
利率僅達 38.9% (QoQ +5.1ppts / YoY -1.7ppts)，低於我們原先及市場預期的 40%水準，主要
受到添鴻產品結構轉差及新廠折舊攤提影響 ；EPS 為 11.18 元 (QoQ -30.6% / YoY +58.6%)，
略低於我們原先預期的 11.73 元。 
3Q26 受惠於 CoWoS 等機台認列數成長及強勁拉貨需求，營收呈現季增，營收達 22.21 億元
(QoQ +28.7%/YoY+48.7%) ；研究部預估毛利率 略為季增至 40.1% (QoQ + 1.2ppts / YoY 
-0.5ppts)，產品組合持續改善，預估 EPS 為 15.86 元(QoQ +41.9%/ YoY +52.5%)。 
展望 4Q26， 受惠於先進封裝及記憶體等強勁需求的持續出機認列，研究部預期營收將持續季增，
預估 4Q26 營收將達 24.87 億元(QoQ +12.0%/YoY+15.7%)，毛利率將持穩於 40.0% (QoQ 
-0.1ppts / YoY -2.1ppts)。整體而言，預估 FY26 全年營收達 80.32 億元(YoY+23.3%)，毛利率
達 38.5%(YoY-2.6 ppts)，EPS 為 60.66 元(YoY+33.5%)。

資料來源：弘塑，國泰證期研究部整理 
至 FY28 出貨量能將持續成長 
研究部認為弘塑設備出貨展望能見度將能延伸至 FY28。由於晶圓廠、OSAT、記憶體客戶皆對先
進封裝設備需求相當迫切，設備交期已長達 10 至 12 個月，惟受限於短期人力供應與廠房籌
備進度，FY26 全年出機量預估維持在 220 至 240 台。為突破產能瓶頸，新竹租賃廠房已展開
重新配置，預計於 2026 年底投產，同時公司亦積極評估購置新竹現成廠房。 
展望至 FY27/FY28，FY27 設備出貨量預估將年增 50%，FY28 亦有機會再成長 50%。至 FY27，
OSAT 客戶的設備訂單將較 2026 年呈現倍數成長，正式接棒成為 FY27 推升公司營運成長的最
大主力。至 FY28 及中長期，受惠於先進封裝發展趨勢明確，除出貨量增加外，SoIC、CPO 及
面板級封裝等封裝技術迭代帶動設備 ASP 顯著提升（SoIC 設備較 CoWoS 設備單價增加 30% 
至 50%，面板級封裝達 2.5 至 3 倍以上），外包比例亦有望因自家產能開出而下降，整體營運呈
現量價齊揚。

### Page 3

添鴻化學品業務將於 1Q27 逐漸看見轉機 
隨 OSAT 客戶於今年 Q2/Q3 進入裝機密集期，新產線仍須經測試與量產爬坡，相關的化學品需
求通常會在設備交機後約 3~4 個月逐步提升，因此短期營收仍受新廠折舊與稼動率偏低影響，
預期添鴻的整體稼動率與毛利率有望在 1Q27 逐漸擺脫谷底，迎來較為明顯的營運轉機。 
在設備出機量持續提升 ， 且化學品業務將於FY27 看見較大轉機 ，研究部預估 FY27 營收達 115.49
億元(YoY+43.8%)。利潤率將受惠於產品組合轉好、外包比例降低而提升，預估 FY27 全年毛利
率達 43.0%(YoY+4.5ppts)，全年 EPS 為 96.33 元(YoY+58.8%)。 
訂單能見度無虞 
展望 2026 年至 2028 年，在各先進封裝平台強勁需求下 ， 設備產能已告全滿 ， 交期長達 10 至 
12 個月，訂單能見度直達 2H27。就產品組合來看，設備業務高階產品比重將逐漸提升，預估
至 FY27 SoIC/CPO 等營收占比將提升至 10~15%， 添鴻化學品也將受惠於OSAT 客戶逐漸提升
拉貨動能，整體營收與獲利成長動能明確。 
研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 60.66 元/96.33 元(YoY＋33.5%/+58.8%)。弘塑目前交易於
35/22 倍之 2026/2027 年獲利預估 。 研究部維持買進投資建議 ， 目標價2,500 元，以 26x FY27 
獲利預估。

公司簡介 
弘塑集團由四家公司組成： 2013 年合併化學品公司添鴻、 2015 年投資日本公司 T azmo/ 
Apprecia、2018 年合併佳霖、收購太引，並投資 SVXR。

### Page 4

ESG 
弘塑 ESG 報告書依循由全球永續性報告協會(Global Reporting Initiative, GRI) 所發布之永續性報
導準則(GRI Standards)之核心選項編撰，同時參考上市上櫃公司永 續發展實務守則及聯合國永續
發展目標，揭露永續發展相關資訊。報告書中所揭  露的統計數據皆由譜瑞各權責單位彙整提供。
財務績效數據係經資誠聯合會計師事務所依照國際財務報導準則 (International Financial 
Reporting Standards, IFRS)查核簽證之年度合併財務報告。 
除了開發具有競爭力的產品外，弘塑亦以實踐永續發展自期，對商業行為採取最  高專業及道德的
標準，善盡對利害關係人與環境的關心與責任，整合公司資源遵守永續性原則，並持續進行各項改
善。 
經濟面： 
A. 股東權益：公司優點為董事長親自出席股東常會；股東會結束後公開揭露逐項議案之詳細
表決資訊。法規遵循方面，公司於題項評估期間未有不合理或受主管機關處分之情事。(例
如：訴訟、未分派股利、未說明受理小股東提案期間、持股質押比逾 50%、庫藏股買回
或私募股票理由不合理之情形)。 
B. 董事會結構與運作：公司優點為公司未有政府機關、屬同集團之公司或財團法人占董事會
席次達三分之一；揭露審計委員會運作情形；針對功能性委員會進行績效評估，並揭露評
估結果。公司可加強處為法定外功能性委員會之獨立董事未達半數，或未揭露該委員會之
組成、職責與運作情形；董事會之外部績效評估頻率未達標準。獨立董事方面，公司優點
為至少 2 名獨立董事之連續任期不超過 9 年。董事會永續治理方面，公司優點為負責永
續發展或企業社會責任的最高負責人為總經理；當年度永續報告書經董事會通過。董事會
績效與薪酬方面，公司可加強處為未揭露董事薪酬與績效評估結果之連結；企業永續績效
尚未與高階主管及董事薪酬進行連結。董事會多元性方面，公司優點為揭露董事會多元化
政策並揭露落實情形；董事會成員具備不同專業背景。 
C. 行為準則與內控：公司優點為制定反貪腐政策；制定檢舉制度並揭露檢舉情形；訂定內部
規則禁止內部人利用公司內部訊息於有價證券市場獲利 。 公司可加強處為未揭露反競爭或
反托拉斯的相關政策。內控單位與人才方面，公司優點為設置誠信經營負責單位；設置公
司治理主管，並說明職權範圍、業務執行重點及進修情形；內部人員有取得內部稽核相關
證照，例如：內部稽核師。法規遵循方面，評估期間公司未違反重大誠信經營與內控準則
等公司治理相關法規。 
D. 風險及危機管理：公司優點為訂立各種類型的風險回應策略；考量營運中對價值鏈所產生
的環境及社會外部性並揭露相關作為。公司可加強處為未揭露風險管控專責單位；未說明
其風險政策經董事會通過。資訊安全方面，公司優點為設置資安長或資訊安全專責單位；
制定資訊管理政策；制定定期檢視、測試資訊安全機制，並說明檢視成果；提供員工資訊
安全相關教育訓練。公司可加強處為未取得資訊安全相關認證；未揭露個資相關政策。 
E. ESG 創新：公司可加強處為未揭露綠色相關部門設置情形與職責；未揭露綠色/永續採購
之政策或金額 。 此外 ， 產業創新方面 ， 公司優點為對其專利之智慧財產權進行管理與保護，
並揭露量化績效。法規遵循方面，公司近 3 年並未受公平交易委員會懲罰。

### Page 5

社會面： 
A. 人權：公司優點為評估可能遭遇的人權議題，及提出因應措施；揭露人權政策執行結果。
法規遵循方面，評估期間未違反人權相關法規。 
B. 員工溝通與福利：公司優點為定期召開勞資會議供員工參與討論其權利和義務；揭露優於
法規的員工休假福利；訂定具體之職業安全衛生政策且明確揭露實際成果；設有職業安全
衛生委員會負責工安事項；針對工傷事件，揭露統計數據；設定減少工安事件的目標。在
工會爭議與職業災害方面，未有重大職業災害情事。 
C. 人力資本發展：公司優點為制定員工績效評估辦法且定期評比；提供員工能力發展培育計
畫；揭露當年度員工訓練的投入時間或成本。公司可加強處為未透過指標衡量員工訓練成
果。人才留任方面，公司優點為揭露新進員工人數或其占總員工人數之比例，並依照不同
指標(如性別、年齡等)區分；揭露員工離職人數、離職率或流動率，並依照不同指標(如性
別、年齡等)區分；公司可加強處為未提及員工認股權、持股信託等股權分享計畫；未揭
露員工滿意度調查結果。 
D. 多元組成與包容性：公司優點為針對員工組成之多元性擬定策略與目標，並揭露員工組成
結構。公司可加強處為未揭露各階級男女薪酬比；未揭露女性主管人數或佔比；未說明少
數族群雇用情形。 
E. 身心障礙員工雇用方面，當年度足額雇用身心障礙員工或針對未足額情形提出改善措施。 
F. 供應鏈社會面向控管：公司優點為詳細說明人權政策包含項目；依循《 RBA 責任商業聯
盟準則》保障供應商或承攬商之人權。公司可加強處為未說明在新供應商遴選標準中的
ESG 社會面準則；未說明是否具負責任礦物採購管理流程， 或要求供應商簽屬相關衝突礦
產聲明書。 
G. 客戶關係管理：公司優點為公司透過專人或管道即時處理客戶之意見、揭露客訴情形及改
善措施；揭露客戶滿意度數據並設定客戶滿意度目標。法規遵循方面，評估期間並未違反
消費者權益相關爭議或法律訴訟，或已針對違反情形進行說明。 
H. 產品安全：公司優點為自行設立實驗室或委託第三方公正實驗室抽樣產品品質；制定產品
製程持續計畫，確保生產計劃不中斷；取得品質相關認證。公司可加強處為未揭露與產品
品質/服務品質相關之教育訓練時數或費用。 
環境面： 
A.  環境系統與治理：公司優點為回覆 CDP 問卷。公司可加強處為未揭露將綠色概念融入營
運過程的作為。 
B. 空氣管理：公司可加強處為未揭露空氣汙染管理專責單位及相關管理政策、未揭露空氣汙
染排放數據、未揭露量化空氣汙染減量目標。法規遵循方面，評估期間未違反空氣汙染相
關法規或已針對違反情形提出具體改善措施。

### Page 6

C. 能源與氣候變遷：公司優點為說明董事會或管理階層真對氣候變遷議題之監督情形；評估
氣候變遷機會。公司可加強處為未揭露使用具公信力的 1.5 °C 以下及 3 °C 以上溫度情
境評估氣候風險；未揭露與供應鏈或合作夥伴共同推動因應氣候變遷相關策略。溫室氣體
管理方面，公司優點為制定溫室氣體管理政策；揭露至少三年的溫室氣體範疇一與二排放
量，並揭露涵蓋範圍或佔營收比率；制定量化溫室氣體減量目標追蹤績效；通過碳盤查認
證；執行碳足跡制度。公司可加強處為未揭露範疇三排放量。節能管理方面，公司優點為
制定節能管理政策；揭露用電或節能數據；進行能源風險評估。公司可加強處為未揭露節
能量化目標。再生能源管理方面，公司優點為制定再生能源管理政策；公司可加強處為未
揭露再生能源使用量或發電量；未揭露量化再生能源發展目標。 
D. 水管理：公司優點為制定廢水管理政策；公司可加強處為未揭露量化廢汙水減量目標。水
資源管理方面，公司優點為制定水資源管理政策、揭露用水來源；公司可加強處為未揭露
量化用水減量目標。法規遵循方面，評估期間未違反水資源相關法規或已針對違反情形提
出具體改善措施。 
E. 原物料與廢棄物管理：公司優點為揭露廢棄物、資源回收總量。公司可加強處為未揭露原
物料使用量；未揭露量化廢棄物減量目標；未揭露量化有毒／有害廢棄物減量目標。法規
遵循方面 ， 評估期間未違反廢棄物及有害物質相關法規或已針對違反情形提出具體改善措
施。 
F. 供應商及產品生命週期管理：公司優點為制定綠色供應鏈管理政策。公司可加強處為公司
未揭露供應鏈環境管理成效。

熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《中東地緣風險升溫不利航空，工業估值仍偏高》
受美伊互相攻擊油輪 、胡塞組織控制曼德海峽及沙
烏地阿拉伯東西向輸油管遇襲關閉等待修復 ，預估
布蘭特油價將維持在 90美元以上較長時間 。近期原
油價格上漲也導致 9/18航空燃油再度攀升到 195美
元，回到3月美伊衝突後的高峰 。由於4Q26油價高
機率將維持在90美元以上，預估將對航空公司運量
與獲利將造成負面衝擊。
雖然機構將工業產業 4Q26、1Q27獲利年增率從
7/1 的 4.3% 、 18.4% 微 幅 上 調 到9/18 的 7.6% 、

20.1%。上半年在美伊衝突下帶動國防類股大漲 ，
帶動工業產業預估本益比達到近30年的高點27倍。
雖然近期工業受到高油價與國防政策利空已經回檔
7%，但9/22預估本益比仍高達 22.6倍，高於過去
五年平均20.9倍，也高於整體標普500的19.1倍。
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望

2
重點摘要
• 油價與運價維持高檔 ，航空
產業需求遭遇逆風
• 眾議院通過限制擴大對伊朗
軍事行動 ，對國防類股形成
壓力
《油價與運價維持高檔，航空產業需求遭遇逆風》
近期受到中東衝突升溫導致僅有少量油輪通過荷姆
茲海峽，貨櫃船實際上仍無法通行。過去5-6年全球
港口吞吐運量成長遠低於船舶訂單量成長 ，導致重
大港口的貨櫃塞港嚴重 。中東地緣政治風險和全球
性的港口擁擠預估短期內都未能改善契機 ，因此未
來半年貨櫃運價仍將維持高位 。由於過去半年美國
機票價格已大幅上漲 ，導致價格敏感的美國旅客消
費開始偏向保守。美國運輸安全管理局(簡稱TSA)統
計每日旅客吞吐量近期呈現持續下滑 ，顯示航空業
持續性漲價已經衝擊到旅客需求 。隨著機構上調

4Q26原油預測 10%到88美元，高價位的航空燃油
價格也將再次對全球航空公司運量與獲利造成衝擊 。
《眾議院通過限制擴大對伊朗軍事行動 ，對國防類
股形成壓力》
今年上半年因中東衝突升溫及美伊緊張情勢 ，國防
股曾出現一波強勁上漲 。但隨著美國眾議院 9/15以
220票對204票通過限制總統對伊朗軍事行動的決議
案，要求未獲國會授權前不得持續或擴大對伊朗的
軍事行動 。雖然美國國會決議案主要是限制新增的
軍事行動授權 ，並非削減國防預算 。但美國國會限
制進一步軍事行動 ，已經造成市場資金對國防類股
進行獲利了結 。考量航空類股仍將面臨高油價逆風 ，
以及美國眾議院擴大限制政府對伊朗軍事行動 。本
行對工業產業展望維持中立評等。
資料來源：TSA、JP Morgan，2026/9/17，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/24

Document 439: 【趨勢主題】歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會

Source PDF: all report pdf/【趨勢主題】歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會.pdf
Report source: CTBC
Date: 2026-10-02

Status: ok

### Page 1

時性和正確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之
需求及投資之風險，並就投資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不
涉及特定投資標的之建議。
歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會
1
Thematic Report
重點提示
中東地緣衝突自2026年2月底爆發以來仍陷僵局，能源價格維
持高檔的時間較預期更長，令歐元區通膨壓力與升息預期升溫。
另一方面，歐洲政府擴大財政支出，公債供給增加，加上歐洲
央行持續縮表，推升期限溢價，令歐洲公債殖利率大幅走升，
德國十年期公債殖利率9/28升破3.6%，創2009年6月以來新高。
本報告透過問答，分析歐洲公債殖利率走高的原因、主權債務
與財政風險、政府因應措施、高利率對企業的影響，以及高利
率環境下的歐洲股債展望。
◼ 本波歐洲公債殖利率走高的驅動因素：能源價格上漲推升通
膨、市場重估歐央貨幣政策路徑，同時，財政支出令歐洲公

債供給增加與歐央持續縮表使私人部門需要吸收更多公債供
給，推升期限溢價，財政疑慮使部分歐洲國家利差承受額外
壓力，加上全球利率上升的外溢影響，共同推升殖利率走高。
◼ 歐洲政府調整發債結構，央行備有穩市工具：歐洲政府縮短
發債期限以控制融資成本，預估此趨勢將延續至2027年；
萬一主權債市場出現無序波動，歐洲央行有OMT與TPI政策
工具，但不會輕易動用。
◼ 整體歐洲債務風險尚可控但中長期壓力持續累積：目前利率
水準尚未對整體債務可持續性構成普遍威脅，但財政脆弱性
較高的國家壓力升高，法國尤其需要關注。財政與政治變數
使法國資產風險溢價維持高位，目前股債定價已反映相當程
度的負面風險。
◼ 德國公債殖利率短期將維持高檔：歐洲央行12月仍有再升息
可能，待能源價格與通膨壓力緩和、升息預期降溫後，利率
才有回落空間，但難降至3%以下。
◼ 歐洲企業資產負債表健康、獲利具韌性，緩衝高利率衝擊。
◼ 高利率限制歐股估值擴張，歐股維持中立，聚焦獲利成長與
政策支撐的產業：銀行受惠於目前利率環境與信貸需求，工

業、電氣化/能源安全及AI受惠戰略自主與資本支出提升。
Sep. 29, 2026
林亞瑱
歐洲總經政策與市場分析

市場重估歐央政策路徑，推升歐洲公債利率走高
2
Thematic Report

￭

、

政策路徑
，

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/15，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/9。
能源價格大漲引發政策路徑重估為利率上揚主因
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/15，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/9。
德國公債殖利率曲線上移，短率升幅大於長率
中東衝突發生以來德國公債殖利率曲線(中東地緣衝突前 vs. 目前)
近期全球公債殖利率大幅上揚 ，歐洲國家亦然，德國10年期公
債殖利率9/28升破3.6%，創2009年6月來新高，若從2月底中

東地緣衝突爆發以來起算 ，德國十年債利率累積彈升了近 100
個基點，30年期德債利率亦逼近4%(至9/28止)。
Q1：什麼因素推動今年歐洲公債殖利率走高？
(1)能源價格上漲推升通膨、市場重估歐央貨幣政策路徑，為驅
動本波利率上揚的主因：
2月底中東衝突爆發以來 ，德國10年期公債殖利率與油氣價格
走勢高度相關。此外，德國公債各期限殖利率全面上移 ，其中
短率升幅高於長率 ，反映本波利率上揚主 要受到能源價格上升
所帶動的通膨風險，以及市場對ECB政策路徑重新定價的影響。
此外，美國殖利率上升透過全球資本市場產生外溢效果 ，帶動
全球無風險利率中樞上移 ，亦對歐洲公債殖利率形成上行壓力 。

■淨債券發行 ■ECB縮表 ◆對私人部門的淨供給
(2)歐洲公債供給增加、央行持續縮表，推升期限溢價：
德國擴大財政支出，歐洲政府融資需求及公債供給增加；同時 ，
ECB持續進行資產負債表正常化 ，逐步減少公債持有量 ，使新
增債券供給需由私人投資人吸收 ，推升投資人要求的期限溢價 。
除此之外，政府債務持有者結構轉變 ，個人與海外投資人等對
價格較為敏感的投資人占比增加，使債市更容易受到市場情緒
與金融條件變化的影響。
(3)財政疑慮使部分歐洲國家利差承受額外壓力：
歐洲部分國家面臨較高的政府債務壓力，加上政治局勢限制財
政調整空間，市場對債務可持續性的疑慮難消 。尤其在年度預
算協商期間，財政脆弱性較高的國家如法國 、英國，主權債務

風險重新定價容易反映在長端殖利率及與德債的利差擴大。

涉及特定投資標的之建議。
Q2：面對公債殖利率上升，歐洲政府如何因應？
面對公債殖利率上升 ，歐洲政府調整發債結構 、增加短年期債
券發行，以控制高利率環境下的融資成本 ，預期此趨勢將延續
至2027年；惟縮短債務期限雖可降低當期融資成本，也將提高
中期再融資風險。
Q3：萬一歐洲主權債市場出現無序波動，歐洲有哪些
政策工具可以運用？
歐洲央行主要政策工具有二 ，分別為直接貨幣交易 (OMT)與傳
導保護工具(TPI)。
(1)直接貨幣交易(OMT)：2012年9月推出，源自歐債危機，可
在次級市場購買符合條件國家的1至3年期政府公債，且購買
規模事前不設上限 ，惟須搭配歐洲穩定機制 (ESM)的金融援
助計畫，並具嚴格的附帶條件，使用門檻較高，由主權國家

主動申請。
(2)傳導保護工具 (TPI)：2022年7月推出 ，可在次級市場購買
符合條件國家的 1至10年期政府部門債券 ，目的在於因應與
該國基本面不相稱的無序市場波動 ，避免金融市場分裂影響
貨幣政策傳導。啟動時將評估該國是否符合歐盟財政規範 、
債務是否具備可持續性等條件 ，由歐央評估後決定是否啟動。
不過，OMT與TPI均屬市場失序時的最後手段 ，並非用於壓低
正常的主權債殖利率或消除國家間的基本面利差 ，啟動有嚴格
條件，歐央不會輕易動用，目前此兩項工具均未實際動用過。
高利率下，歐洲政府調整發債、央行備有穩市工具

Q4：歐洲政府債務的可持續性是否有立即風險？
主要歐洲國家除德國與西班牙外 ，IMF預估2026年政府債務佔
GDP比重均超過 100%。同時，近年地緣衝突頻率升高 ，戰略
自主與強化地區安全已成為歐洲核心目標 。為了讓成員國有擴
大國防支出的空間 ，歐盟允許成員國啟動 國家豁免條款，得以
在增加國防支出的同時 ，暫時不受歐盟財政規則約束 ，截至目
前已有18個歐盟會員國申請。國防支出提升，若未搭配增加財
政收入或其他支出調整，將使政府債務維持在較高路徑。
所幸，從利率與經濟成長率差距來看 ，目前利率水準尚未對歐
洲整體債務可持續性構成 立即威脅，但部分高負債國家的債務
壓力仍須留意。過去十年低利率環境的效益逐步消退 ，利率與

經濟成長率的差距 (r－g)較過去不利 ，加上期限溢價回升 ，使
高負債國家的債務動態面臨風險。
檢視歐元區四大經濟體 ，德國、西班牙政府債務的利率低於經
濟成長率，債務動態相對穩健；義大利政府債務 佔GDP比重最
高，所幸在政府控制支出下，自2024年以來維持初級財政盈餘，
有助控制債務比率進一步擴大。法國利率雖略低於經濟成長率 ，
但政府債務已接近 GDP的120%，在財政赤字偏高 、利息支出
持續增加且財政整頓進展有限 的情況下，未來兩年債務佔GDP
比率預期將持續上升，財政脆弱性相對較高。
整體歐洲債務風險可控但中長期壓力正在累積

Q5：近期法德利差顯著擴大，驅動因素與對法國股債
市的影響？
財政與政治不確定性升溫 ，推升法國主權風險溢價 ：法國步入
財政與政治不確定性升溫時期 ，不利的財政前景 ，加上4Q26
預算協商與2027年將舉行總統大選，政治不確定性升溫推升十
年期法德利差9月下旬突破100個基點，9/28升至112個基點、
創2012年6月以來新高。法國總統大選將於2027年4~5月舉行，
4/18舉行第一輪投票 ，若無候選人過半 ，5/2將舉行第二輪投
票。由於法國政治版圖高度分裂 、且極右派支持度領先 ，相較
於過往選舉周期，2027年法國選舉的不確定更高，預估將令市
場在2027年總統大選前維持謹慎情緒。
民調反映政治版圖分裂 ，增加財政政策的不確定性： 民調顯示，
極右派國民聯盟候選人瑪琳·勒龐在總統大選首輪的支持度領先，
超過三成，極左派法國不屈黨候選人梅朗雄 、中間偏右候選人
愛德華·菲利普民調落在15~18%左右。法國大選高機率將會進
入第二輪投票，取決於進入第二輪的候選人 ，目前民調顯示以

中間偏右候選人菲利普最有希望擊敗勒龐 ，若為極左派梅朗雄
進入第二輪，則勒龐勝選機率較高。

股債市影響：財政與政治變數將令法國資產風險溢價維持高位
(1)債市： 巴克萊資本 (2026/8)預估三種情境： (a)正向情境：
中東衝突告一段落 、秋季預算協商順利 、中間派候選人民調上
升，十年期法德利差有機會收斂至60~65個基點；(b)不利情境：
秋季預算協商引發市場雜音 、中東衝突進一步推高升息預期 ，
總統大選民調維持較高不確定性 ，法 德 利差 預 估 將 擴 大 至

95~100個基點；(c)極端情境：沒有中間派候選人進入第二輪
總統大選，法德利差可能突破100個基點。
9/28十年期法德利差已升至 112個基點，顯示市場已反映相當
程度的政治風險，再大幅惡化的空間應有限 。儘管如此，不確
定性短期難消，預估法德利差仍將維持在(b)情境高檔盤整。
(2)股市：法國股市估值相較歐股處於折價，且投資人對法國股
市的配置已明顯降低 ，有助緩和股市大幅修正的風險 。然而，
短線亦缺乏驅動股市行情的催化劑 ，法國經濟成長預測持續下
修、經濟動能落後歐元區 ，加上政治不確定性將影響投資人配
置意願，預估法國股市在2027年4月總統大選前波動仍大 ，金
融與內需導向型企業估值預估將承壓。

Q6：德債利率後續走勢？
貨幣政策正常化與結構因素 ，德債利率中樞上移： 低實質利率
環境已改變，根據歐洲央行執委Schnabel(2025/2)分析，過去
壓低實質利率的部分結構性因素正 在逆轉。一方面，地緣政治
碎片化、氣候變遷及勞動力短缺 ，使中長期通膨上行風險增加；
另一方面，全球由「儲蓄過剩」逐步轉向「債券供給過剩 」，
政府財政赤字擴大與央行資產負債表正常化 ，使安全資產供給
增加、稀缺性下降 ，進而推升實質利率 。疫情後實質利率上升
究竟是暫時現象 ，抑或代表新的長期均衡目前雖難預測 ，但從
全球儲蓄與投資平衡 的變化，地緣政治與氣候變遷等結構挑戰 ，
實質利率上升似乎是較有可能出現的情況。
利率短期維持高檔 ，待通膨與升息預期緩和才有回落空間： 能
源價格衝擊與通膨傳導將是牽動後續利率的主要因素 。歐央
2026年已升息二碼，存款機制利率上調至2.5%的中性區間上緣。

中東衝突持續下 ，歐央上調2027~2028年通膨預期 ，預計中期
內通膨率和核心通膨率將高於2%政策目標。目前天然氣期貨價
格已偏離歐央基準情境 、往不利情境靠攏 ，成品油價格因裂解
利差跳升而居高不下 ，因此，除非中東局勢迅速平息 ，否則預
估12月將再升一碼至 2.75%。9/28利率期貨反映 市場預期2027
年9月前將再升息超過3碼。不過，高能源價格若 延續較久，將
抑制經濟成長 ，殖利率上升也將發揮緊縮效果 、削弱通膨傳導 ，
我們預期歐央持續升息的必要性低於目前市場定價 。後續利率
上行空間將取決於能源價格與通膨是否進一步超出預期 ，待通
膨與升息預期緩和 ，利率將有回落空間 。惟考量結構性因素推
升，德國10年期公債殖利率預期將維持在3%以上；若要回落至
3%以下，可能需要看到歐央再次調降政策利率 ，預估4Q27才
有機會降息。

### Page 9

需求及投資之風險，並就投資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不

Q7：高利率對歐洲企業的影響？
首先，歐洲企業資產負債表整體維持健康 ，歐洲企業近年持續
去槓桿，淨債務/EBITDA比率維持相對低檔 ，整體財務槓桿不
高。而且，企業債務期限普遍較長 ，平均到期期限約五年 ，高
利率對企業利息負擔的傳導較為緩慢 ，因此，即使公債殖利率

上升，企業債務成本的重新定價將逐步反映 ，降低企業對利率
上升的敏感度。
另一方面 ，企業信貸需求仍具韌性 ，8月歐元區非金融企業貸
款年增4.2%，顯示截至目前企業融資活動並未因利率走高而明
顯降溫。此外，儘管歐洲公債殖利率大幅走高 ，歐洲投資級債
及非投資級債信用利差並未明顯擴大 ，顯示企業信用基本面 並
未出現明顯惡化。
整體而言，企業資產負債表維持健康 ，融資需求仍具韌性 ，信
用風險未明顯升高，高利率目前對企業的直接衝擊有限。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2005年以來至2026/9/23，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/9。

Q8：歐股展望？
2026年以來至9/28止，歐洲道瓊50指數上漲9%，過去一個月
出現回檔 ，能源價格再度上漲 、TTF天然氣期貨價格飆升至三
年多高點，央行立場偏鷹、公債殖利率大幅走高 ，加上歐洲政
治雜音增加，均對盤勢形成壓力 。然而，歐股自高點以來波段
回檔不到5%、幅度可控，企業獲利維持韌性扮演重要緩衝。
STOXX歐洲600企業2026年上半年每股盈餘較去年同期成長

14%，創近3年來最快增速 。雖然獲利成長主要由商品類股貢
獻，但商品以外類股上半年每股盈餘年增率亦上修至 7%。高
盛證券(2026/8)預估，2026年下半年歐洲企業獲利年增率將進
一步加速至19%，全年獲利預估成長15%。
短線歐股盤勢將受到油價與利率牽動 ，過去一個月德國十年期
公債殖利率跳升 36個基點 ，壓抑歐股評價 ，預估本益比降至
14.7倍(9/28)。目前美伊仍透過斡旋進行接觸 ，惟尚未看到達
成和解的明確路徑 ，在能源價格維持高檔 、通膨上行風險仍存
之下，歐央可能進一步升息，此外，2027年歐洲多國進入選舉
周期，政治不確定性將干擾市場情緒 ，令本益比擴張空間受限。
2027年企業獲利增速預估將由 2026年的高雙位數放緩至中個
位數，但仍維持穩定成長 ，將為股市提供基本面支撐 ，惟股市
動能預期將較為溫和。
Q9：高利率環境下，歐股布局聚焦哪些方向？
利率維持高檔、指數評價擴張空間有限下 ，歐股展望維持中立 ，
相對看好具獲利成長或政策支撐的產業 。銀行股受惠於目前利

率環境及企業信貸需求回升；工業 /國防、電氣化/能源安全及
AI等概念股則受惠於政策支持及相關投資需求增加。

銀行股受惠目前利率環境與信貸需求回升
銀行股獲利穩健，且在殖利率上升環境表現相對較佳 。歐洲央
行政策利率維持在 2%–3%區間，對銀行業有利 ；企業信貸需
求回升，有助銀行淨利息收入維持成長 ，摩根大通(2026/9)預
估，2026、2027年歐洲銀行NII將分別成長9%及6%。此外，
歐洲銀行CET1資本比率維持高檔 ，反映銀行具備充足的資本
緩衝。在上述因素支撐下 ，預估歐洲銀行2025~ 2028年提存

前營業利益年均複合成長8.8%，銀行獲利具韌性。
歐洲強化戰略自主，相關產業受益資本支出提升
歐洲強化戰略自主，工業/國防、電氣化/能源安全及AI等領域
受政策支持，將帶動相關投資需求 。隨著德國財政刺激逐步落
地，國防、基礎建設、能源及AI投資推進，有助支撐相關產業
的獲利動能。相較之下，能源價格維持高檔更久 ，對消費支出
構成風險，令消費股面臨逆風 ，需待地緣衝突緩解 。從獲利動
能來看，戰略自主受惠產業獲利動能持續優於消費類股 ，顯示
具政策與投資需求支撐的戰略產業獲利展望相對明確。

Document 459: パリは燃えている、揺れるフランス政治 ～大規模抗議が揺さぶる財政再建と大統領選挙～

Source PDF: all report pdf/パリは燃えている、揺れるフランス政治 ～大規模抗議が揺さぶる財政再建と大統領選挙～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

Europe Trends

2026 年10 月 19 日

1/6 
パリは燃えている、揺れるフランス政治 
～大規模抗議が揺さぶる財政再建と大統領選挙～ 
 経済調査部 首席エコノミスト  田中 理 
（℡：050-5474-7494）

（要旨） 
○ フランスで学生の抗議運動を発端とする大規模なデモが広がり、一部で暴動に発展している。
教育環境の悪化、物価高による生活の厳しさ、公共サービスの質の低下が、政府への不信と
結びつき、労働組合や年金生活者など幅広い層を巻き込む社会運動に拡大した。 
○ 政府は 2027 年予算案で 540 億ユーロの財政改善措置を計画するが、歳出抑制への反発が強
まれば、野党との妥協は一段と難しくなる。抗議の長期化による経済活動の下押しや、金利
上昇による利払い負担の増加が、財政再建を一段と難しくする恐れもある。予算成立の遅れ
や財政再建策の後退は、国債市場の警戒感を高めかねない。 
○ 来春の大統領選に向けても、購買力や公共サービスの改善に加え、暴動への対応や治安維持
が重要な政策争点となる可能性がある。各党は政府の財政運営への批判を強める一方、予算
不成立による混乱をどこまで容認するかという難しい判断を迫られる。 
○ 今回の大規模抗議運動は、財政再建の必要性が高まる一方で、その負担を国民に求めるため
の政治的・社会的な合意形成が難しくなっているという構造的な問題を浮き彫りにした。来
春の大統領選で誰が政権を担うことになっても、財政規律の回復と国民生活の改善をいかに
両立させるかという難題から逃れることはできない。

■学生の抗議から全国的な社会運動へ 
フランスで大規模な抗議運動が広がっている。10 月 17 日には全国約110 ヶ所でデモが行われ、
参加者は内務省の集計で約55 万人、主催者側の発表では約80 万人に達した。パリだけでも約11
万人が参加し、リヨンやマルセイユなど主要都市でも大規模な抗議活動が行われた。デモの多く
は平和的に実施されたが、覆面姿の集団など一部が暴徒化し、警察と激しく衝突した。商店の略
奪や建物への放火が発生し、地方都市では市庁舎など公共施設も被害を受けた。警察による拘束
者は全国で2000 人を超え、警察官らにも多数の負傷者が出た。マクロン大統領は18 日夜、国民
向けの演説を行い、生活費や教育環境の改善を求める抗議に理解を示す一方、暴力行為を厳しく
非難した。政府は被害を受けた自治体や商店への緊急支援を進めるとともに、必要に応じて一部

### Page 2

2/6
の集会を禁止するなど、治安対策を強化する。社会的な不満への対応と公共秩序の回復を同時に
迫られる事態となっている。
発端となったのは、９月中旬にパリ郊外の学校で始まった高校生らによる抗議活動だった。一
部の学校では、新学期が始まっても担当教員が決まらず、授業を開始できなかったり、病気や休
職で教員が不在になっても代替教員を確保できず、授業が休講になったりする問題が発生してい
る。休講が重なることで授業の進度が遅れ、学力の習得や進学に向けた準備に支障が生じる。校
舎の老朽化や教育設備の不足、学級規模の大きさなどにも不満が広がっている。
こうした教育環境の改善を求める抗議活動は、ソーシャルメディアを通じて広がり、他の地域
の高校生や大学生も加わった。フランスの教育への公的支出は国際的にみて必ずしも低いわけで
はない。それにもかかわらず、学校現場での教員不足や施設の老朽化が問題となるのは、教員の
地域的な偏在や教育資源の配分など、構造的な要因があると考えられる。
特に生活費や住居費が高いパリ周辺や、低所得世帯が集中する地域では、教員の確保が難し

い。教員の給与水準は、同程度の学歴を持つ他の就業者に比べて低いことも、教職の魅力を損ね
る要因となっている。また、学校施設の整備については、中央政府だけでなく地方自治体も重要
な役割を担っており、小学校は市町村、中学校は県、高校は地域圏が主に所管している。老朽化
した校舎の改修に加えて、猛暑対策や省エネルギー改修にも対応する必要があり、地方自治体の
財政負担は大きい。家庭の経済環境による教育格差も大きい。所得水準の低い地域では、学習支
援や進学機会にも制約があり、教育機会の不平等が固定化しやすい。今回の抗議運動は、教育へ
の財政投下が学校現場の環境改善や格差縮小に十分につながっていないことが、不満の背景にあ
ると考えられる。
学生を中心に始まった抗議運動は、社会の幅広い層に広がっている。10 月17 日のデモには、主
要な労働組合の関係者、2018 年に大規模な抗議運動を展開した「黄色いベスト運動」の参加者、
年金生活者、農業従事者、環境団体、左派政党の関係者などが加わった。それぞれの要求は必ず

しも一致しないが、生計費負担の増加や公共サービスの質の低下、政府の経済・社会政策への不
満という点で共通している。
新型コロナ危機後の供給制約やロシアによるウクライナ侵攻後のエネルギー価格の高騰に端を
発した生計費危機の影響は今も続いている。物価上昇率がピークアウトした後も、伸び率が鈍化
しただけで、その後も物価は上がり続けている。そこにイラン情勢の緊迫化に伴う原油や天然ガ
ス価格の再高騰が加わり、家計負担が再び高まっている。特に低所得者層や若年層では、食料
品、燃料費、住居費などが支出全体に占める割合が高く、物価高の影響を受けやすい。世論は今
のところ抗議活動を概ね支持している。調査会社Elabeが10 月 16 日に公表した世論調査では、生
活費高騰に対する抗議運動に支持や共感を示した回答者は69％に達した。
今後の焦点は、抗議運動が持続的な社会運動に発展するかである。主要労働組合は11 月５日に
全国的なストライキ・デモを実施することを発表している。賃上げや公共サービスの充実、雇用

維持などを掲げ、政府・民間の労働者に幅広く参加を呼び掛けている。同日は国民議会（下院）
で 2027 年予算案の歳出部分が審議される予定の時期と重なる。主要労働組合の共同歩調が続き、

### Page 3

3/6 
抗議活動がさらに広がれば、歳出抑制策を巡って政府が野党と妥協する際の政治的な制約が強ま
る可能性がある。また、ストライキが長期化すれば、経済・社会活動の下押しを通じて、景気や
税収への悪影響が広がる恐れがある。

■政府予算案の国民負担の増加に強まる反発 
今回の大規模な抗議運動は、政府が財政再建を目指す重要な時期に発生した。ルコルニュ政権
が 10 月１日に議会へ提出した2027 年予算案では、2026 年に国内総生産（ＧＤＰ）比で 5.4％へ
膨らむ見通しの財政赤字を、2027 年に同5.0％に縮小することを目指している。そのために必要
とされる財政改善措置は約540 億ユーロに達し、このうち約430 億ユーロを新たな予算措置で確
保する計画だ。フランスは主要先進国の中でも税・社会保障負担が重く、さらなる増税による企
業の競争力低下を避けるため、政府案は歳出抑制に重点を置いている。公務員給与の算定基準の
凍結、一定額を超える年金給付の物価連動の停止、医療・社会保障支出の抑制、地方自治体への
歳出削減要求などが盛り込まれた。国防など一部の優先分野では歳出を拡大する一方、その他の
分野では歳出の伸びを抑制する。 
問題は、巨額の財政改善措置を講じても、財政赤字の改善幅がＧＤＰ比で僅か0.4 ポイントに
とどまることである。現行制度の下で見込まれる歳出の増加や金利上昇に伴う利払い費の拡大が
財政を圧迫しているためだ。財政・政治懸念の高まりによる金利上昇が、景気の冷え込みや利払
い費の更なる増加を招く恐れがあり、財政政策の自由度は一段と低下している。 
 今回の抗議運動は、こうした歳出抑制策への政治的な抵抗を強める可能性がある。政府が予算
成立に向けた野党の協力を取り付けたり、国民の不満を和らげたりするために、公共サービスや
社会保障関連の歳出削減を緩和すれば、十分な代替財源を確保できない限り、財政再建が遠退
く。他方で、財政再建を優先して厳しい歳出抑制策を維持すれば、抗議運動が長期化するリスク
がある。政府は財政規律の回復と社会的な合意形成の間で難しい判断を迫られている。 
 ルコルニュ政権にとって最大の難関は、国民議会で過半数の議席を確保していないことだ。
2027 年予算を通常の議会審議によって成立させるには、野党の一部から協力を得る必要がある。
予算成立の鍵を握るのは、穏健左派の社会党（ＰＳ）と極右政党・国民連合（ＲＮ）の対応であ
る。両党は政府予算案に反対する立場では共通するが、求める政策修正の方向性が異なる。社会
党は富裕層・大企業への増税や社会保障給付の維持を求める一方、国民連合は移民関連支出や欧
州連合（ＥＵ）への拠出金の削減、行政経費の削減などを通じた財政再建を主張している。企業
の税負担軽減や物価対策の強化も掲げているが、ＥＵ拠出金の削減などには制度上の制約もあ
り、財政改善計画の実現可能性は疑わしい。 
 社会党は前年度の予算審議で、年金改革の停止などの政策的譲歩を引き出し、他の野党が提出
した内閣不信任案への賛成を見送ることで、予算成立と政権延命に協力した。だが、今年度の予
算協議では、マクロン大統領の目玉政策であった年金改革停止のような大きな譲歩を勝ち取るこ
とが難しい。来春に大統領選挙を控えており、政府への協力に慎重になっている。一方、国民連
合のルペン氏は、予算不成立による混乱が長引くよりも、不完全でも予算を成立させる方が望ま

### Page 4

4/6 
しいとの認識を示している。10 月13 日には、社会党が提出した政府予算案を本格審議前に退ける
動議に国民連合が反対票を投じ、政府は極右政党の協力で最初の関門を乗り切った。 
野党の協力が得られない場合、政府が憲法で認められた特別な立法手続きを利用する可能性も
ある。憲法49 条３項に基づく手続きでは、予算案の議会採決を迂回できるが、その場合、議会は
内閣不信任案を提出でき、不信任案が否決されれば法案は採択されたものと見做され、可決され
れば内閣は総辞職する。また、憲法47 条では、議会が予算案の提出から70 日以内に審議を終え
なければ、政府はオルドナンス（行政命令）によって予算を執行できると定められている。この
手法は現在の政治体制（第五共和制）下で使われた前例がなく、実施すれば大きな政治的な反発
を招く恐れがある。年内の予算成立が難しい場合、最低限の税徴収や行政サービスを継続する特
別法を国民議会で可決する選択肢もある。こうした手段を用いれば、野党との全面的な合意がな
くても予算措置を実施できる余地はある。だが、49 条３項や特別法の手続きでも、野党の部分的
な協力が必要で、政策的な譲歩の過程で財政再建が遠退く可能性がある。 
今後の予算審議は、10 月 20 日に歳入部分、27 日に社会保障予算案、11 月 17 日に予算案全体の
採決が予定されている。ただし、審議の進展や採決の結果次第で、日程は変更される可能性があ
る。まずは歳入部分の審議で政府が社会党や国民連合からどの程度の協力を取り付けることがで
きるかが最初の重要な関門となろう。 
金融市場にとって重要なのは、予算が成立するかどうかだけではない。野党との妥協によって
歳出抑制が緩和される場合も、実効性のある代替財源を確保できれば、必ずしも財政赤字目標の
達成が難しくなるとは限らない。反対に、実現可能性が不透明な歳出削減や増税に依存すれば、
見かけ上の財政改善額が確保されても、財政運営に対する信頼は回復しにくい。予算を成立させ
るための妥協と、財政再建策の実効性をどう両立させるかが問われている。

■大統領選では購買力と治安が二つの争点に 
 今回の抗議運動は、2027 年春の大統領選挙にも影響を及ぼす可能性がある。学生運動に端を発
した抗議活動は、生活費の高騰や公共サービスの劣化に対する国民の不満を浮き彫りにした。一
方、10 月17 日のデモでは一部が暴動に発展し、商店の略奪や放火被害が広がったことを受け、治
安や公共秩序の維持も重要な政治課題となっている。大統領選では、購買力の改善や公共サービ
スの充実に加え、暴力行為への対応や警察権限のあり方を巡って、各候補者の政策上の違いが明
確になる可能性がある。 
 今回の抗議運動に積極的に関与しているのが、極左政党・不服従のフランス（ＬＦＩ）のメラ
ンション氏だ。同氏は学生による抗議活動をいち早く支持し、10 月 17 日のデモにも参加した。教
育や医療などの公共サービスへの支出拡大、富裕層への増税、年金制度の見直しなどを訴える一
方、警察による抗議活動への対応を批判している。ただし、一部の参加者による暴力行為を巡っ
ては、他の政治勢力からＬＦＩへの批判も強まっており、平和的な抗議への支持と公共秩序の維
持をどう両立させるかが問われている。 
これに対し、国民連合のルペン氏は、生活費負担の軽減を訴える一方、暴動を厳しく批判し、

### Page 5

5/6 
治安対策の強化を重視している。今回の抗議運動に対する共感は左派支持層だけでなく国民連合
の支持層にも広がっているが、学生運動そのものへの支持には大きな違いがある。国民連合にと
っても、購買力対策と治安維持をどう具体的な政策に結びつけるかが重要な論点となる。 
大統領選に向けた各政治勢力の再編も進んでいる。10 月 11 日には、欧州議会議員のグリュック
スマン氏が社会党を中心とする社会民主勢力の統一候補に選出され、メランション氏が率いる不
服従のフランスとの選挙協力に慎重な姿勢を示している。一方、中道勢力ではフィリップ元首相
やアタル元首相らの候補者調整がなお課題となっている。最近の世論調査では、ルペン氏が初回
投票で３割超の支持を集める一方、メランション氏、グリュックスマン氏、フィリップ氏が二番
手争いを繰り広げている。支持率は候補者の組み合わせによって異なり、今回の暴動後の変化も
現時点では十分確認できない。 
来春の大統領選が近づくことは、今後の予算協議を複雑にする。社会党は政府との妥協を通じ
て政策修正を実現することと、野党としての立場を維持することの間で判断を迫られる。一方、
国民連合も政府予算案への反対姿勢を示しながら、予算不成立による混乱をどこまで容認するか
という問題に直面している。各党の対応は、単なる予算案への賛否にとどまらず、財政責任と政
府への対抗姿勢をどのように両立させるかという問題でもある。 
さらに重要なのは、次期政権がどのような経済・財政政策を掲げる場合でも、厳しい財政制約
から逃れられないことである。公共サービスの拡充や購買力対策を進めるには財源が必要となる
一方、歳出削減や増税を進めれば社会的反発を招く可能性がある。大統領選では各候補者の政策
公約だけでなく、その財源や実行可能性が問われることになる。

■財政再建と社会的合意形成の両立が最大の課題 
今回の抗議運動は、2018 年の黄色いベスト運動と共通する特徴を持つ。特定の政策や生活上の
不満を出発点とする抗議が、政府の経済・社会政策全般への不信に結びつき、全国規模の運動へ
広がったことだ。ただし、燃料税引き上げへの反発から始まった黄色いベスト運動とは異なり、
今回は教育環境の改善を求める学生が運動の発端となった。若者の将来不安に、物価高や公共サ
ービスの劣化に対する幅広い層の不満が結びついた点に特徴がある。今後の焦点は、学生と労働
組合の連携がどこまで持続するか、11 月５日に予定される全国的なストライキ・デモがどの程度
の規模となるかである。 
抗議運動の長期化は、2027 年予算の成立を一段と難しくする可能性がある。政府は歳出抑制を
中心とする財政再建を目指しているが、野党の協力なしに予算を成立させることは難しい。社会
党は社会保障給付や公共サービスへの支出抑制に反対し、国民連合もＥＵの拠出金や移民関連支
出の削減を中心とした財政再建策を要求している。大規模な抗議活動が続けば、野党が政府に歳
出削減策の修正を求める圧力が強まろう。政府が野党の要求に応じれば、予算成立に近づく一
方、十分な代替財源を確保しない限り、財政再建が遠退く。他方、憲法49 条３項などを利用して
議会採決を回避しようとすれば、内閣不信任や更なる社会的反発を招く恐れがある。 
金融市場への波及にも注意が必要だ。フランスでは財政悪化と政治的不透明感を背景に国債が

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
売られ、10 年物国債利回りは10 月中旬に５％近くまで上昇した。ドイツ国債との利回り格差（ス
プレッド）も一時150bpを超え、財政再建の実行可能性に対する市場の不安が高まっている。予算
協議の混乱や財政再建策の後退が現実味を増せば、財政運営への信認が損なわれ、国債利回りに
さらなる上昇圧力がかかる可能性がある。国債利回りの上昇は、新規発行や借り換えを通じて政
府の利払い費を押し上げ、財政再建を一段と難しくする。国債市場の不安定化が政府の財政運営
を制約し、それが政治的な不満や予算協議の難航につながるような、悪循環にも注意が必要だ。 
来春に大統領選挙を控えていることも、予算協議の先行きを複雑にしている。各党は政府の財
政運営への批判を強める一方、予算不成立による政治的混乱をどこまで容認するかという判断を
迫られる。選挙戦では、購買力の改善、公共サービスの充実、治安の回復などが重要な争点とな
り、各候補者が独自の政策を打ち出すとみられる。しかし、財政余力が乏しいなかでは、歳出拡
大や減税を掲げても、その財源をどう確保するかという問題を避けて通れない。各党が掲げる財
政再建策の実現可能性も、選挙後の政策運営を考えるうえで重要となる。 
 フランスでは財政赤字と公的債務が膨らみ、財政再建を先送りする余地は狭まっている。しか
し、社会保障や公共サービスへの歳出抑制を進めようとすれば国民の反発が強まり、議会での合
意形成が難しくなる。今回の大規模抗議運動は、財政再建の必要性が高まる一方で、その負担を
国民に求めるための政治的・社会的な合意形成が難しくなっているという、フランスが抱える構
造的な問題を改めて浮き彫りにした。来春の大統領選で誰が政権を担うことになっても、財政規
律の回復と国民生活の改善をいかに両立させるかという難題から逃れることはできない。

Document 478: 中国製ハイブリッド車の輸入制限に踏み切るEU

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国製ハイブリッド車の輸入制限に踏み切るEU.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 20 日

MURC Focus

中国製ハイブリッド車の輸入制限に踏み切る EU

～日本にとっても“対岸の火事”ではない～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 12 月にセーフガードを発動 
欧州連合（EU）は 10 月 12 日、輸入が急増している中国製のハイブリッド車（HV）とプラグインハイブリッド車
（PHV）を対象に、12 月よりセーフガード（緊急輸入制限）を発動すると発表した。期間は4 年間で、1 年目は HV
と PHV の年間輸入台数を計 35 万台とし、その後の増加幅は年間 6%までに制限する。EU 籍のメーカーが中
国で生産する車も対象となるが、中国メーカーの EU 域内での生産車は対象外となる。

EU は 2024 年 10 月 30 日より、中国製 EV（電気自動車）に対し、基本関税 10%に加えて、最大で 35.3%の
追加関税を課している。廉価な中国製 EV の輸入を抑制する目的からだが、一方で HV と PHV には追加関税
を課してこなかった。その結果、中国製 HV と PHV の輸入が 2025 年から急増し、EU 製の HV と PHV は劣勢
に追いやられた（図表 1）。また電動車の対中貿易収支も、HV と PHV を中心に赤字が膨らんだ（図表 2）。 
図表 1. 中国製電動車の輸入台数の推移 図表 2. 電動車の対中貿易収支

（注）EV:電気自動車、PHV:プラグインハイブリッド車、HV:ハイブリッド車
